### P028 — Propagate change impact across technical relationships

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Change closes locally while dependent evidence remains green and stale.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence-bearing impact analysis that admits uncertainty and feeds implementation, integration and revalidation—not a form approval.
- **TRIGGER:** High coupling; shared resources; reused assets; safety/performance effects; cross-organisation changes.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For a local isolated change, deterministic dependency analysis plus focused regression is the cheap path.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority to baseline, change, waive, release, roll back and supersede must be explicit and consequence-tiered.
- **CRITICISMS:** Dependency maps are incomplete/stale; impact tools produce false positives; exhaustive traversal can exceed change value.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** CCBs, forms and baseline reviews are optional. The property is truthful identity, authority, implementation status and evidence continuity.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S080`, `S081`, `S082`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S081`, `S082`, `S080`, `S003`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Governed items, stable identifiers, change authority, status accounting and access to actual product/model/test/environment state.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Configuration must be tied to the authorised capability, commitments and operational population.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** This family centrally requires exact item/composition/version/environment/evidence identity.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Interface and supplier configuration dependencies must be included in impact and release decisions.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Evidence must cite configuration and be invalidated or justified after change.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Traceability completeness versus maintenance burden** — balancing properties: P044, P052; hybrid: Consumer-defined relation types, automation with semantic checks, expiry and pruning.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Propagate change impact across technical relationships” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can change closes locally while dependent evidence remains green and stale remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Can the exact model, implementation, test, data, tool and environment configuration supporting a claim be reconstructed?
- Does change impact invalidate and re-establish all affected evidence rather than merely record approval?

- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For a local isolated change, deterministic dependency analysis plus focused regression is the cheap path.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence-bearing impact analysis that admits uncertainty and feeds implementation, integration and revalidation—not a form approval.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Propagate change impact across technical relationships
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Named question, decision or evidence use; no value claim from existence or volume.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority is claim- and use-specific, never automatic truth.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Permissible decisions, assumptions, uncertainty, fidelity and caveats.
- **PROVENANCE:** Model/metamodel/tool/version, data and transformations are identified.
- **PARITY:** Implementation/physical/test/environment relations and permitted divergence are explicit.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Semantic mapping and transformation losses are tested; open export/API and exit path considered.
- **VALIDATION:** Credibility evidence proportionate to decision consequence.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Refresh latency, event triggers and stale-state signalling are explicit.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For a local isolated change, deterministic dependency analysis plus focused regression is the cheap path.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence-bearing impact analysis that admits uncertainty and feeds implementation, integration and revalidation—not a form approval.

</details>

### P029 — Re-establish evidence currentness after change

- **FAILURE_MODE:** A system appears assured because historical evidence remains marked complete.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence is a versioned claim with an applicability boundary and refresh rule, not a timeless attachment.
- **TRIGGER:** Any claim carried across versions; certification; reused analyses/models; operational updates.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** No-op or provably irrelevant changes can retain evidence with a concise impact proof.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority to baseline, change, waive, release, roll back and supersede must be explicit and consequence-tiered.
- **CRITICISMS:** Over-invalidation wastes effort; under-invalidation creates latent false assurance; impact proofs can be self-serving.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** CCBs, forms and baseline reviews are optional. The property is truthful identity, authority, implementation status and evidence continuity.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S017`, `S081`, `S084`, `S120`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S081`, `S003`, `S017`, `S084`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Governed items, stable identifiers, change authority, status accounting and access to actual product/model/test/environment state.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Configuration must be tied to the authorised capability, commitments and operational population.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** This family centrally requires exact item/composition/version/environment/evidence identity.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Interface and supplier configuration dependencies must be included in impact and release decisions.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Evidence must cite configuration and be invalidated or justified after change.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Configuration control versus rapid adaptation** — balancing properties: P044, P051; hybrid: Tiered authority; automated status/impact checks; emergency path; rollback and post-change reconciliation.
- **Evidence reuse versus stale-assumption risk** — balancing properties: P050; hybrid: Explicit applicability envelope, invalidation rules, delta analysis and revalidation.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Re-establish evidence currentness after change” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can a system appears assured because historical evidence remains marked complete remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Can the exact model, implementation, test, data, tool and environment configuration supporting a claim be reconstructed?
- Does change impact invalidate and re-establish all affected evidence rather than merely record approval?

### P030 — Maintain parity among model, implementation, test, data and environment

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Analysis and review are correct for a representation that no longer governs reality.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Governed representation parity with explicit, risk-bounded inconsistencies—never an unsupported claim that one repository equals reality.
- **TRIGGER:** MBSE/digital thread; generated artefacts; AI/ML datasets; simulation; multi-tool programmes.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For a compact code-centric system, source, dependency lock, tests and environment manifest may form the whole parity set.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority to baseline, change, waive, release, roll back and supersede must be explicit and consequence-tiered.
- **CRITICISMS:** Strict global consistency can be infeasible; synchronisation can dominate engineering time; transformations may preserve syntax but not meaning.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** CCBs, forms and baseline reviews are optional. The property is truthful identity, authority, implementation status and evidence continuity.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S016`, `S017`, `S071`, `S094`, `S097`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S071`, `S094`, `S097`, `S016`

- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Interface and supplier configuration dependencies must be included in impact and release decisions.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Evidence must cite configuration and be invalidated or justified after change.

### P031 — Plan integration strategy, sequence and readiness

- **FAILURE_MODE:** The first system-level composition occurs after architecture and supplier choices are hard to change.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A living risk/learning strategy that determines what is combined next and why, rather than a date-driven final-assembly schedule.
- **TRIGGER:** Multiple components/suppliers; scarce testbeds; hardware/software coupling; high integration risk.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Two-component low-risk work may integrate directly under a short checklist.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **CRITICISMS:** Detailed plans become obsolete; readiness criteria can be gamed; schedule pressure encourages paper waivers.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Integration plans and readiness reviews are optional carriers. The property is early enough, risk-ranked composition evidence.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S042`, `S083`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S042`, `S003`, `S083`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Defined interfaces/configurations, integration authority, suitable environments, entry evidence, anomaly process and feasible sequencing.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration must exercise the mission/use interactions that matter, not only convenient assembly paths.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Every integration result must identify products, software/data, environment, tools and configuration.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Risky semantic, timing, physical, organisational and state interfaces are first-class targets.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration evidence is not automatically verification or validation; claims, coverage and limitations must be stated.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Early commitment versus option preservation** — balancing properties: P019, P021; hybrid: Set-based maturation, staged commitments, option value, decision deadlines and reopen triggers.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Plan integration strategy, sequence and readiness” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can the first system-level composition occurs after architecture and supplier choices are hard to change remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Does integration begin at the riskiest boundaries while options remain, or only after component completion?
- Do integration anomalies feed back to the requirement, architecture or assumption that produced them?

- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Owned, current, linked to the decision and given reopening/falsification triggers.
- **AUTHORITY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **CONFIGURATION:** Architecture decision and views/models identify the applicable product/model/configuration.
- **EVALUATION:** Scenario, analysis, prototype, model, review or integration evidence proportional to consequence.
- **REOPEN_TRIGGER:** Changed intent, invalidated assumption, margin erosion, interface/integration evidence, new alternative or configuration drift.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Two-component low-risk work may integrate directly under a short checklist.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A living risk/learning strategy that determines what is combined next and why, rather than a date-driven final-assembly schedule.

- **SEMANTICS:** Units, ranges, meaning, timing, failure/degraded behaviour and provenance are explicit at risk-appropriate precision.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Cross-boundary assumptions are owned, current and testable/monitorable.
- **VERSION_IDENTITY:** Endpoint, contract, tool/data and environment versions are bound to evidence.
- **CHANGE_NOTIFICATION:** Change impact, compatibility window and revalidation responsibilities are explicit.
- **VERIFICATION:** Check each side's conformance to the contract.
- **INTEGRATION:** Exercise composition and end-to-end interactions in a credible context.
- **DEGRADATION:** Fallback, error handling and residual incompatibility are represented where consequential.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Two-component low-risk work may integrate directly under a short checklist.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A living risk/learning strategy that determines what is combined next and why, rather than a date-driven final-assembly schedule.

#### CONFIGURATION_CHANGE_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Plan integration strategy, sequence and readiness
- **IDENTIFIED_ITEMS:** All representations/products/environments whose identity can change the claim or decision.
- **BASELINE_OR_CURRENT_STATE:** Authoritative current composition and supersession are explicit; a baseline is not presumed frozen forever.
- **CHANGE_AUTHORITY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **IMPACT_SCOPE:** Requirements, architecture, assumptions, interfaces, suppliers, models, code/hardware, tests, operations and claims as material.
- **STATUS_ACCOUNTING:** Decision, implementation, rollout, exception and actual operational state are distinguished.

- **CHEAP_PATH:** Two-component low-risk work may integrate directly under a short checklist.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A living risk/learning strategy that determines what is combined next and why, rather than a date-driven final-assembly schedule.

- **PROPERTY:** Plan integration strategy, sequence and readiness
- **INTEGRATION_OBJECTIVE:** Expose the highest-value composition uncertainty early enough to change design/action.
- **SEQUENCE:** Risk-, dependency- and learning-ordered rather than merely schedule-ordered.
- **ENTRY_IDENTITY:** Exact component/model/software/data/environment versions and readiness evidence.
- **ENVIRONMENT_OR_SURROGATE:** Fidelity, intended use, omissions and correlation are explicit.
- **INTERFACE_TARGETS:** Semantic, timing, state, physical, organisational and failure interactions.
- **ANOMALY_FLOW:** Observation, configuration, hypothesis, disposition, waiver and regression remain linked.

- **CHEAP_PATH:** Two-component low-risk work may integrate directly under a short checklist.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A living risk/learning strategy that determines what is combined next and why, rather than a date-driven final-assembly schedule.

#### VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Plan integration strategy, sequence and readiness
- **CLAIM:** A specific requirement, architecture/interface proposition, model-use claim, mission/use claim or change-equivalence claim.
- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does it fulfil the current intended use/need in the relevant context?
- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Two-component low-risk work may integrate directly under a short checklist.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A living risk/learning strategy that determines what is combined next and why, rather than a date-driven final-assembly schedule.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Plan integration strategy, sequence and readiness
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Named question, decision or evidence use; no value claim from existence or volume.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority is claim- and use-specific, never automatic truth.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Permissible decisions, assumptions, uncertainty, fidelity and caveats.
- **PROVENANCE:** Model/metamodel/tool/version, data and transformations are identified.
- **PARITY:** Implementation/physical/test/environment relations and permitted divergence are explicit.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Semantic mapping and transformation losses are tested; open export/API and exit path considered.
- **VALIDATION:** Credibility evidence proportionate to decision consequence.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Refresh latency, event triggers and stale-state signalling are explicit.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Two-component low-risk work may integrate directly under a short checklist.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A living risk/learning strategy that determines what is combined next and why, rather than a date-driven final-assembly schedule.

### P032 — Integrate early, incrementally and at risky boundaries

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Composition defects drive rework, delay and blame across organisational boundaries.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Integration cadence proportional to risk and reversibility, with escalating fidelity and truthful limits.
- **TRIGGER:** Novel interactions; distributed teams; change-prone software; high coupling; long supplier chains.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where physical integration is destructive or extraordinarily costly, use earlier models/fixtures and fewer evidence-rich physical events.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **CRITICISMS:** Continuous integration rhetoric can ignore hardware lead times, unavailable environments and low-fidelity simulator error.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Integration plans and readiness reviews are optional carriers. The property is early enough, risk-ranked composition evidence.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S037`, `S042`, `S047`, `S083`, `S120`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S037`, `S042`, `S083`, `S047`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Defined interfaces/configurations, integration authority, suitable environments, entry evidence, anomaly process and feasible sequencing.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration must exercise the mission/use interactions that matter, not only convenient assembly paths.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Every integration result must identify products, software/data, environment, tools and configuration.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Risky semantic, timing, physical, organisational and state interfaces are first-class targets.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration evidence is not automatically verification or validation; claims, coverage and limitations must be stated.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Independent review versus rapid feedback** — balancing properties: P058; hybrid: Standing automated checks plus targeted independent challenge at high-risk claims/transitions.
- **Process depth versus small/reversible work** — balancing properties: P044, P058; hybrid: Explicit activation thresholds, cheap path, progressive escalation and control retirement.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Integrate early, incrementally and at risky boundaries” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can composition defects drive rework, delay and blame across organisational boundaries remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Does integration begin at the riskiest boundaries while options remain, or only after component completion?
- Do integration anomalies feed back to the requirement, architecture or assumption that produced them?

### P033 — Use credible integration environments, stubs, simulators and testbeds

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Early integration passes and later high-fidelity integration fails.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Decision-bounded integration surrogates with explicit credibility and migration to higher fidelity.
- **TRIGGER:** Unavailable/expensive components; hazardous tests; distributed delivery; autonomy; rare environments.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use real components directly where cheap and safe; do not build a simulator merely because the process expects one.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **CRITICISMS:** Simulator construction and maintenance can become a parallel system project; common-mode model error can mislead all teams.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Integration plans and readiness reviews are optional carriers. The property is early enough, risk-ranked composition evidence.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S016`, `S017`, `S083`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S016`, `S003`, `S017`, `S083`

- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Every integration result must identify products, software/data, environment, tools and configuration.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Risky semantic, timing, physical, organisational and state interfaces are first-class targets.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration evidence is not automatically verification or validation; claims, coverage and limitations must be stated.

- **DECISION:** Define surrogate purpose, fidelity, assumptions, interfaces, known omissions and correlation evidence; retire or upgrade when it no longer answers the decision.
- **ALTERNATIVES:** Materially different feasible options, including status quo/reference where informative; no token alternatives.
- **RATIONALE:** Decision context, criteria, evidence, uncertainty, rejected alternatives and consequences.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Owned, current, linked to the decision and given reopening/falsification triggers.
- **AUTHORITY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **CONFIGURATION:** Architecture decision and views/models identify the applicable product/model/configuration.
- **EVALUATION:** Scenario, analysis, prototype, model, review or integration evidence proportional to consequence.
- **REOPEN_TRIGGER:** Changed intent, invalidated assumption, margin erosion, interface/integration evidence, new alternative or configuration drift.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use real components directly where cheap and safe; do not build a simulator merely because the process expects one.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Decision-bounded integration surrogates with explicit credibility and migration to higher fidelity.

#### INTERFACE_ASSUMPTION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Use credible integration environments, stubs, simulators and testbeds
- **INTERFACE_SCOPE:** Physical, logical, data, timing, state/mode, protocol, organisational, authority and environmental dimensions as applicable.
- **OWNER:** Both endpoints plus joint resolution/escalation authority; a document custodian alone is insufficient.
- **SEMANTICS:** Units, ranges, meaning, timing, failure/degraded behaviour and provenance are explicit at risk-appropriate precision.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Cross-boundary assumptions are owned, current and testable/monitorable.
- **VERSION_IDENTITY:** Endpoint, contract, tool/data and environment versions are bound to evidence.
- **CHANGE_NOTIFICATION:** Change impact, compatibility window and revalidation responsibilities are explicit.
- **VERIFICATION:** Check each side's conformance to the contract.
- **INTEGRATION:** Exercise composition and end-to-end interactions in a credible context.
- **DEGRADATION:** Fallback, error handling and residual incompatibility are represented where consequential.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use real components directly where cheap and safe; do not build a simulator merely because the process expects one.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Decision-bounded integration surrogates with explicit credibility and migration to higher fidelity.

#### CONFIGURATION_CHANGE_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Use credible integration environments, stubs, simulators and testbeds
- **IDENTIFIED_ITEMS:** All representations/products/environments whose identity can change the claim or decision.
- **BASELINE_OR_CURRENT_STATE:** Authoritative current composition and supersession are explicit; a baseline is not presumed frozen forever.
- **CHANGE_AUTHORITY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **IMPACT_SCOPE:** Requirements, architecture, assumptions, interfaces, suppliers, models, code/hardware, tests, operations and claims as material.
- **STATUS_ACCOUNTING:** Decision, implementation, rollout, exception and actual operational state are distinguished.
- **EVIDENCE_CURRENTNESS:** Evidence cites configuration and has invalidation/reuse rules.
- **ROLLBACK_OR_RECOVERY:** Required where change consequence and reversibility justify it.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use real components directly where cheap and safe; do not build a simulator merely because the process expects one.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Decision-bounded integration surrogates with explicit credibility and migration to higher fidelity.

#### INTEGRATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Use credible integration environments, stubs, simulators and testbeds
- **INTEGRATION_OBJECTIVE:** Expose the highest-value composition uncertainty early enough to change design/action.
- **SEQUENCE:** Risk-, dependency- and learning-ordered rather than merely schedule-ordered.
- **ENTRY_IDENTITY:** Exact component/model/software/data/environment versions and readiness evidence.
- **ENVIRONMENT_OR_SURROGATE:** Fidelity, intended use, omissions and correlation are explicit.
- **INTERFACE_TARGETS:** Semantic, timing, state, physical, organisational and failure interactions.
- **ANOMALY_FLOW:** Observation, configuration, hypothesis, disposition, waiver and regression remain linked.
- **REGRESSION:** Selected from change impact and prior failure mechanisms.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use real components directly where cheap and safe; do not build a simulator merely because the process expects one.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Decision-bounded integration surrogates with explicit credibility and migration to higher fidelity.

#### VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Use credible integration environments, stubs, simulators and testbeds
- **CLAIM:** A specific requirement, architecture/interface proposition, model-use claim, mission/use claim or change-equivalence claim.
- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does it fulfil the current intended use/need in the relevant context?
- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use real components directly where cheap and safe; do not build a simulator merely because the process expects one.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Decision-bounded integration surrogates with explicit credibility and migration to higher fidelity.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Use credible integration environments, stubs, simulators and testbeds
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Named question, decision or evidence use; no value claim from existence or volume.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority is claim- and use-specific, never automatic truth.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Permissible decisions, assumptions, uncertainty, fidelity and caveats.
- **PROVENANCE:** Model/metamodel/tool/version, data and transformations are identified.
- **PARITY:** Implementation/physical/test/environment relations and permitted divergence are explicit.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Semantic mapping and transformation losses are tested; open export/API and exit path considered.
- **VALIDATION:** Credibility evidence proportionate to decision consequence.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Refresh latency, event triggers and stale-state signalling are explicit.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use real components directly where cheap and safe; do not build a simulator merely because the process expects one.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Decision-bounded integration surrogates with explicit credibility and migration to higher fidelity.

</details>

### P034 — Manage integration anomalies and regressions as system evidence

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Contradictory evidence is normalised until an operational failure exposes it.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Anomaly closure means explanatory and evidential resolution, not status colour change.
- **TRIGGER:** Complex integration; intermittent failures; safety/mission consequence; reused evidence; many configurations.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** A reproducible low-risk defect can be fixed and regression-tested without a formal board.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **CRITICISMS:** Queues and boards can delay obvious fixes; teams may game severity or classify away inconvenient evidence.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Integration plans and readiness reviews are optional carriers. The property is early enough, risk-ranked composition evidence.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S045`, `S046`, `S083`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S046`, `S045`, `S003`, `S083`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Defined interfaces/configurations, integration authority, suitable environments, entry evidence, anomaly process and feasible sequencing.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration must exercise the mission/use interactions that matter, not only convenient assembly paths.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Every integration result must identify products, software/data, environment, tools and configuration.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Risky semantic, timing, physical, organisational and state interfaces are first-class targets.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration evidence is not automatically verification or validation; claims, coverage and limitations must be stated.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- No specific ledger tension mapped; generic cost/benefit and tailoring tension still applies.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Manage integration anomalies and regressions as system evidence” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can contradictory evidence is normalised until an operational failure exposes it remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Does integration begin at the riskiest boundaries while options remain, or only after component completion?
- Do integration anomalies feed back to the requirement, architecture or assumption that produced them?

### P035 — Reason about emergent and whole-system interactions

- **FAILURE_MODE:** All allocated requirements pass yet mission behaviour is unstable, unsafe or ineffective.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Whole-system interaction hypotheses tied to testable evidence and boundaries, not vague complexity language.
- **TRIGGER:** Complex adaptive systems; autonomy; dense coupling; SoS; humans in operation; uncertain environment.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Do not invoke 'emergence' for every dependency: deterministic interface and resource checks should be exhausted first.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **CRITICISMS:** Emergence can become an excuse for poor specification or unfalsifiable modelling; exhaustive prediction is impossible.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Integration plans and readiness reviews are optional carriers. The property is early enough, risk-ranked composition evidence.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S089`, `S091`, `S092`, `S093`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S091`, `S089`, `S092`, `S093`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Defined interfaces/configurations, integration authority, suitable environments, entry evidence, anomaly process and feasible sequencing.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration must exercise the mission/use interactions that matter, not only convenient assembly paths.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Every integration result must identify products, software/data, environment, tools and configuration.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Risky semantic, timing, physical, organisational and state interfaces are first-class targets.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration evidence is not automatically verification or validation; claims, coverage and limitations must be stated.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Decomposition versus emergent interactions** — balancing properties: P015; hybrid: Decompose ownership but preserve interaction models, mission threads, composition tests and system-level measures.
- **Modularity versus cross-cutting optimisation** — balancing properties: P016, P055; hybrid: Explicit coupling budgets; platform rules; exceptions with rationale; system-level optimisation and integration evidence.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Reason about emergent and whole-system interactions” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can all allocated requirements pass yet mission behaviour is unstable, unsafe or ineffective remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Does integration begin at the riskiest boundaries while options remain, or only after component completion?
- Do integration anomalies feed back to the requirement, architecture or assumption that produced them?

- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Do not invoke 'emergence' for every dependency: deterministic interface and resource checks should be exhausted first.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Whole-system interaction hypotheses tied to testable evidence and boundaries, not vague complexity language.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Reason about emergent and whole-system interactions
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Named question, decision or evidence use; no value claim from existence or volume.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority is claim- and use-specific, never automatic truth.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Permissible decisions, assumptions, uncertainty, fidelity and caveats.
- **PROVENANCE:** Model/metamodel/tool/version, data and transformations are identified.
- **PARITY:** Implementation/physical/test/environment relations and permitted divergence are explicit.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Semantic mapping and transformation losses are tested; open export/API and exit path considered.
- **VALIDATION:** Credibility evidence proportionate to decision consequence.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Refresh latency, event triggers and stale-state signalling are explicit.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Do not invoke 'emergence' for every dependency: deterministic interface and resource checks should be exhausted first.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Whole-system interaction hypotheses tied to testable evidence and boundaries, not vague complexity language.

</details>

### P036 — Distinguish verification from validation

- **FAILURE_MODE:** A conforming system fails its mission, or an attractive demonstration conceals technical nonconformance.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Two explicit assurance questions with distinct evidence and consumers, applied iteratively across levels.
- **TRIGGER:** Any consequential system; especially where requirements may be wrong, incomplete or stale.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For tiny direct-user work, one feedback event may simultaneously inform both questions, but the two claims remain distinct.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **CRITICISMS:** Terminology varies across domains; ritual definitions do not prevent teams from verifying the wrong baseline.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Compliance matrices, test reports and V-model diagrams are optional. The property is bounded evidence for a specified claim and use.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S015`, `S084`, `S085`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S015`, `S003`, `S084`, `S085`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Explicit claims, authoritative baseline, fit-for-purpose environment/data, independent or competent evaluators and anomaly/limitation handling.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Validation claims require current intended use and representative stakeholders/operations.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Claim, item configuration, procedure/model/tool/data and environment must be fixed and recoverable.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** System and cross-boundary claims require composition evidence, not only component results.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** The property itself requires explicit evidence adequacy, coverage, uncertainty, residual limitations and currentness.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- No specific ledger tension mapped; generic cost/benefit and tailoring tension still applies.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Distinguish verification from validation” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can a conforming system fails its mission, or an attractive demonstration conceals technical nonconformance remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Can the target distinguish verification of specified behaviour from validation of intended-use fitness?
- Is each evidence item valid for this exact claim, configuration, environment and use?

### P037 — Plan V&V and testability before implementation closes options

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Late test design reveals ambiguous claims, inaccessible states, missing instrumentation or unaffordable environments.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Early assurance design that changes requirements and architecture when evidence would otherwise be impossible or noncredible.
- **TRIGGER:** High test cost; hidden internal states; qualification environments; autonomy; supplier evidence.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For straightforward observable behaviour, write the direct check at implementation time.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **CRITICISMS:** Upfront V&V plans can freeze immature requirements and become stale; testability can distort useful design.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Compliance matrices, test reports and V-model diagrams are optional. The property is bounded evidence for a specified claim and use.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S015`, `S030`, `S099`, `S120`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S099`, `S015`, `S030`, `S003`

### P038 — Select evidence methods fit to the claim and decision

- **FAILURE_MODE:** A verification matrix is complete but evidentially weak.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Claim-centred evidence design with explicit adequacy, limitations and residual uncertainty; no method hierarchy is universal.
- **TRIGGER:** Large requirement sets; hazardous/expensive testing; analytical certification; model-based evidence.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use the cheapest deterministic discriminator that genuinely settles the claim.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **CRITICISMS:** Analysis may inherit model assumptions; demonstrations are cherry-pickable; tests sample finite conditions; inspections check only observable attributes.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Compliance matrices, test reports and V-model diagrams are optional. The property is bounded evidence for a specified claim and use.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S016`, `S017`, `S084`, `S086`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S016`, `S017`, `S084`, `S086`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Explicit claims, authoritative baseline, fit-for-purpose environment/data, independent or competent evaluators and anomaly/limitation handling.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Validation claims require current intended use and representative stakeholders/operations.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Claim, item configuration, procedure/model/tool/data and environment must be fixed and recoverable.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** System and cross-boundary claims require composition evidence, not only component results.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** The property itself requires explicit evidence adequacy, coverage, uncertainty, residual limitations and currentness.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG

- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Verification breadth versus cost and latency** — balancing properties: P044; hybrid: Claim- and change-specific V&V; risk-based sampling; layered evidence; staged rollout.
- **Evidence reuse versus stale-assumption risk** — balancing properties: P050; hybrid: Explicit applicability envelope, invalidation rules, delta analysis and revalidation.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Select evidence methods fit to the claim and decision” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can a verification matrix is complete but evidentially weak remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Can the target distinguish verification of specified behaviour from validation of intended-use fitness?
- Is each evidence item valid for this exact claim, configuration, environment and use?

#### STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PROFILE

- **AUTHORITY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.

- **CURRENTNESS:** Re-establish after mission, authority, use-context, interface or configuration change.
- **VALIDATION:** Confirm that the governing stakeholder/mission claim remains legitimate and current enough for the decision.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use the cheapest deterministic discriminator that genuinely settles the claim.

- **PROPERTY:** Select evidence methods fit to the claim and decision
- **NEED_OR_SOURCE:** Must resolve to current stakeholder/mission evidence or an explicitly authorised constraint; for rejected candidates, this is the missing stronger relation.
- **REQUIREMENT_FORM:** Structured text, model, contract, scenario, constraint, testable property or other fit-for-purpose representation; no mandatory template.

- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Material assumptions are explicit, owned and linked to falsification/expiry conditions.

- **VERIFICATION_PRECONDITION:** The claim and applicable configuration are precise enough for a discriminating evidence method.

- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use the cheapest deterministic discriminator that genuinely settles the claim.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Claim-centred evidence design with explicit adequacy, limitations and residual uncertainty; no method hierarchy is universal.

- **APPLICABILITY:** SUPPORTING_OR_NOT_PRIMARY_PROFILE: this property does not create an independent obligation in this profile; apply only where its stated trigger intersects the profile.
- **DRIVERS:** Large requirement sets; hazardous/expensive testing; analytical certification; model-based evidence.

- **RATIONALE:** Match claim type, consequence, observability, uncertainty and environment to test, analysis, inspection, demonstration or combined evidence; justify substitutions and independence.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Explicit claims, authoritative baseline, fit-for-purpose environment/data, independent or competent evaluators and anomaly/limitation handling.
- **AUTHORITY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **CONFIGURATION:** Claim, item configuration, procedure/model/tool/data and environment must be fixed and recoverable.
- **EVALUATION:** The property itself requires explicit evidence adequacy, coverage, uncertainty, residual limitations and currentness.
- **REOPEN_TRIGGER:** Reopen when the source, assumption, interface, configuration or evidence boundary changes materially.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use the cheapest deterministic discriminator that genuinely settles the claim.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Claim-centred evidence design with explicit adequacy, limitations and residual uncertainty; no method hierarchy is universal.

- **SEMANTICS:** Represent semantics only where cross-boundary meaning or behaviour changes the property's claim.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Explicit claims, authoritative baseline, fit-for-purpose environment/data, independent or competent evaluators and anomaly/limitation handling.

- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use the cheapest deterministic discriminator that genuinely settles the claim.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Claim-centred evidence design with explicit adequacy, limitations and residual uncertainty; no method hierarchy is universal.

#### CONFIGURATION_CHANGE_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Select evidence methods fit to the claim and decision
- **IDENTIFIED_ITEMS:** All representations/products/environments whose identity can change the claim or decision.
- **BASELINE_OR_CURRENT_STATE:** Authoritative current composition and supersession are explicit; a baseline is not presumed frozen forever.
- **CHANGE_AUTHORITY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **IMPACT_SCOPE:** Requirements, architecture, assumptions, interfaces, suppliers, models, code/hardware, tests, operations and claims as material.

- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use the cheapest deterministic discriminator that genuinely settles the claim.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Claim-centred evidence design with explicit adequacy, limitations and residual uncertainty; no method hierarchy is universal.

#### INTEGRATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Select evidence methods fit to the claim and decision
- **INTEGRATION_OBJECTIVE:** Expose the highest-value composition uncertainty early enough to change design/action.
- **SEQUENCE:** Risk-, dependency- and learning-ordered rather than merely schedule-ordered.
- **ENTRY_IDENTITY:** Exact component/model/software/data/environment versions and readiness evidence.
- **ENVIRONMENT_OR_SURROGATE:** Fidelity, intended use, omissions and correlation are explicit.
- **INTERFACE_TARGETS:** Semantic, timing, state, physical, organisational and failure interactions.
- **ANOMALY_FLOW:** Observation, configuration, hypothesis, disposition, waiver and regression remain linked.
- **REGRESSION:** Selected from change impact and prior failure mechanisms.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use the cheapest deterministic discriminator that genuinely settles the claim.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Claim-centred evidence design with explicit adequacy, limitations and residual uncertainty; no method hierarchy is universal.

#### VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Select evidence methods fit to the claim and decision
- **CLAIM:** A specific requirement, architecture/interface proposition, model-use claim, mission/use claim or change-equivalence claim.
- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does it fulfil the current intended use/need in the relevant context?
- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use the cheapest deterministic discriminator that genuinely settles the claim.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Claim-centred evidence design with explicit adequacy, limitations and residual uncertainty; no method hierarchy is universal.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Select evidence methods fit to the claim and decision
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Named question, decision or evidence use; no value claim from existence or volume.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority is claim- and use-specific, never automatic truth.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Permissible decisions, assumptions, uncertainty, fidelity and caveats.
- **PROVENANCE:** Model/metamodel/tool/version, data and transformations are identified.
- **PARITY:** Implementation/physical/test/environment relations and permitted divergence are explicit.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Semantic mapping and transformation losses are tested; open export/API and exit path considered.
- **VALIDATION:** Credibility evidence proportionate to decision consequence.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Refresh latency, event triggers and stale-state signalling are explicit.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Use the cheapest deterministic discriminator that genuinely settles the claim.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Claim-centred evidence design with explicit adequacy, limitations and residual uncertainty; no method hierarchy is universal.

### P039 — Validate with real users, operations and mission context

- **FAILURE_MODE:** The system is technically accepted but unusable, mistrusted or ineffective in operation.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Continuous, context-aware validation connected to authoritative need and post-deployment evidence.
- **TRIGGER:** Human-facing systems; new workflows; mission capability; autonomous/AI systems; complex operations.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For a small direct-user service, observe actual use and incorporate rapid feedback rather than stage a formal validation event.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **CRITICISMS:** Operational tests can still be politically scripted, unrepresentative or too late; user preference is not the only system constraint.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Compliance matrices, test reports and V-model diagrams are optional. The property is bounded evidence for a specified claim and use.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S050`, `S085`, `S111`, `S114`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S050`, `S085`, `S111`, `S114`

- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Validation claims require current intended use and representative stakeholders/operations.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Claim, item configuration, procedure/model/tool/data and environment must be fixed and recoverable.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** System and cross-boundary claims require composition evidence, not only component results.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** The property itself requires explicit evidence adequacy, coverage, uncertainty, residual limitations and currentness.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

### P040 — Preserve evidence identity, coverage, limitations and anomalies

- **FAILURE_MODE:** A green compliance matrix overstates what was actually shown.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence as a reproducible, bounded claim rather than a status cell or signed attachment.
- **TRIGGER:** Certification, long lifecycle, reused tests, many variants, distributed test organisations.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For low-risk work, a reproducible command, immutable result and concise limitation note may be enough.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **CRITICISMS:** Evidence records can become huge and inaccessible; metadata completeness can distract from weak discrimination.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Compliance matrices, test reports and V-model diagrams are optional. The property is bounded evidence for a specified claim and use.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S015`, `S046`, `S084`, `S086`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S046`, `S015`, `S003`, `S084`

- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Validation claims require current intended use and representative stakeholders/operations.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Claim, item configuration, procedure/model/tool/data and environment must be fixed and recoverable.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** System and cross-boundary claims require composition evidence, not only component results.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** The property itself requires explicit evidence adequacy, coverage, uncertainty, residual limitations and currentness.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG

- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

- **APPLICABILITY:** SUPPORTING_OR_NOT_PRIMARY_PROFILE: this property does not create an independent obligation in this profile; apply only where its stated trigger intersects the profile.
- **DRIVERS:** Certification, long lifecycle, reused tests, many variants, distributed test organisations.
- **DECISION:** Verifier, validator, qualification/certification authority, acceptance authority, architecture/change authority and operator.
- **ALTERNATIVES:** Do not invent an architecture decision unless the property changes a consequential option.
- **RATIONALE:** Store the claim, method, configuration, environment, data provenance, result, coverage, limitations, reviewer/authority and anomalies as one evidence record.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Explicit claims, authoritative baseline, fit-for-purpose environment/data, independent or competent evaluators and anomaly/limitation handling.
- **AUTHORITY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **CONFIGURATION:** Claim, item configuration, procedure/model/tool/data and environment must be fixed and recoverable.
- **EVALUATION:** The property itself requires explicit evidence adequacy, coverage, uncertainty, residual limitations and currentness.
- **REOPEN_TRIGGER:** Reopen when the source, assumption, interface, configuration or evidence boundary changes materially.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For low-risk work, a reproducible command, immutable result and concise limitation note may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence as a reproducible, bounded claim rather than a status cell or signed attachment.

#### INTERFACE_ASSUMPTION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Preserve evidence identity, coverage, limitations and anomalies
- **APPLICABILITY:** SUPPORTING_OR_NOT_PRIMARY_PROFILE: this property does not create an independent obligation in this profile; apply only where its stated trigger intersects the profile.
- **INTERFACE_SCOPE:** System and cross-boundary claims require composition evidence, not only component results.
- **OWNER:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **SEMANTICS:** Represent semantics only where cross-boundary meaning or behaviour changes the property's claim.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Explicit claims, authoritative baseline, fit-for-purpose environment/data, independent or competent evaluators and anomaly/limitation handling.
- **VERSION_IDENTITY:** Claim, item configuration, procedure/model/tool/data and environment must be fixed and recoverable.
- **CHANGE_NOTIFICATION:** Notify affected owners where change can invalidate compatibility, evidence or authority.
- **VERIFICATION:** The property itself requires explicit evidence adequacy, coverage, uncertainty, residual limitations and currentness.
- **INTEGRATION:** System and cross-boundary claims require composition evidence, not only component results.
- **DEGRADATION:** Represent fallback or residual incompatibility only where consequential.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For low-risk work, a reproducible command, immutable result and concise limitation note may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence as a reproducible, bounded claim rather than a status cell or signed attachment.

#### CONFIGURATION_CHANGE_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Preserve evidence identity, coverage, limitations and anomalies
- **IDENTIFIED_ITEMS:** All representations/products/environments whose identity can change the claim or decision.
- **BASELINE_OR_CURRENT_STATE:** Authoritative current composition and supersession are explicit; a baseline is not presumed frozen forever.
- **CHANGE_AUTHORITY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **IMPACT_SCOPE:** Requirements, architecture, assumptions, interfaces, suppliers, models, code/hardware, tests, operations and claims as material.
- **STATUS_ACCOUNTING:** Decision, implementation, rollout, exception and actual operational state are distinguished.
- **EVIDENCE_CURRENTNESS:** Evidence cites configuration and has invalidation/reuse rules.
- **ROLLBACK_OR_RECOVERY:** Required where change consequence and reversibility justify it.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For low-risk work, a reproducible command, immutable result and concise limitation note may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence as a reproducible, bounded claim rather than a status cell or signed attachment.

#### INTEGRATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Preserve evidence identity, coverage, limitations and anomalies
- **INTEGRATION_OBJECTIVE:** Expose the highest-value composition uncertainty early enough to change design/action.
- **SEQUENCE:** Risk-, dependency- and learning-ordered rather than merely schedule-ordered.
- **ENTRY_IDENTITY:** Exact component/model/software/data/environment versions and readiness evidence.
- **ENVIRONMENT_OR_SURROGATE:** Fidelity, intended use, omissions and correlation are explicit.
- **INTERFACE_TARGETS:** Semantic, timing, state, physical, organisational and failure interactions.
- **ANOMALY_FLOW:** Observation, configuration, hypothesis, disposition, waiver and regression remain linked.
- **REGRESSION:** Selected from change impact and prior failure mechanisms.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For low-risk work, a reproducible command, immutable result and concise limitation note may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence as a reproducible, bounded claim rather than a status cell or signed attachment.

#### VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Preserve evidence identity, coverage, limitations and anomalies
- **CLAIM:** A specific requirement, architecture/interface proposition, model-use claim, mission/use claim or change-equivalence claim.
- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does it fulfil the current intended use/need in the relevant context?
- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For low-risk work, a reproducible command, immutable result and concise limitation note may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence as a reproducible, bounded claim rather than a status cell or signed attachment.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Preserve evidence identity, coverage, limitations and anomalies
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Named question, decision or evidence use; no value claim from existence or volume.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority is claim- and use-specific, never automatic truth.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Permissible decisions, assumptions, uncertainty, fidelity and caveats.
- **PROVENANCE:** Model/metamodel/tool/version, data and transformations are identified.
- **PARITY:** Implementation/physical/test/environment relations and permitted divergence are explicit.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Semantic mapping and transformation losses are tested; open export/API and exit path considered.
- **VALIDATION:** Credibility evidence proportionate to decision consequence.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Refresh latency, event triggers and stale-state signalling are explicit.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For low-risk work, a reproducible command, immutable result and concise limitation note may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence as a reproducible, bounded claim rather than a status cell or signed attachment.

</details>

### P041 — Establish model credibility for a specified intended use

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Decision risk is hidden behind impressive simulation output.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Risk- and use-bounded model credibility with explicit prohibition against unsupported reuse.
- **TRIGGER:** Models replace or shape costly tests; autonomous systems; digital twins; safety/performance decisions.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For low-consequence exploratory models, label them as exploratory and avoid formal credibility programmes.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **CRITICISMS:** Credibility assessment can become expensive paperwork; physical validation data may be scarce or unavailable.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Compliance matrices, test reports and V-model diagrams are optional. The property is bounded evidence for a specified claim and use.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S016`, `S017`, `S099`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S099`, `S016`, `S017`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Explicit claims, authoritative baseline, fit-for-purpose environment/data, independent or competent evaluators and anomaly/limitation handling.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Validation claims require current intended use and representative stakeholders/operations.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Claim, item configuration, procedure/model/tool/data and environment must be fixed and recoverable.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** System and cross-boundary claims require composition evidence, not only component results.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** The property itself requires explicit evidence adequacy, coverage, uncertainty, residual limitations and currentness.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Model automation versus analyst judgement** — balancing properties: P047, P057; hybrid: Automation proposes/checks; humans own authority, assumptions, semantics and consequential acceptance; sampled audits.
- **Evidence reuse versus stale-assumption risk** — balancing properties: P050; hybrid: Explicit applicability envelope, invalidation rules, delta analysis and revalidation.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Establish model credibility for a specified intended use” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can decision risk is hidden behind impressive simulation output remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Can the target distinguish verification of specified behaviour from validation of intended-use fitness?
- Is each evidence item valid for this exact claim, configuration, environment and use?

### P042 — Track technical performance, margins, reserves and trends for decisions

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Teams report green current values while trends or margins make future failure likely.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A small, governed set of leading technical indicators with uncertainty and explicit response—not metric volume.
- **TRIGGER:** Tightly constrained physical systems; shared resources; performance-sensitive architecture; long integration.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Skip dashboards for unconstrained variables; track only measures that can change a decision.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Thresholds, reserve policy, risk acceptance and corrective-action authority must be named.
- **CRITICISMS:** Budgets can be padded or gamed; aggregate margin may conceal local incompatibility; dashboard maintenance can become ceremony.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Dashboards and risk registers are optional. The property is decision-bearing measurement, learning and contingency.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S047`, `S088`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S088`, `S047`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Authoritative objectives, measurable variables, causal understanding sufficient to select indicators and owners with authority to act.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Measures/risks must connect to mission or stakeholder outcomes and constraints.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Measure definition, data source, configuration, time and uncertainty must be stable enough for comparison.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Shared budgets and cross-boundary risks require allocation and integration visibility.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Measures and risk-retirement evidence need calibrated data, trend and uncertainty; scores alone are not evidence.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_TO_STRONG
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** HIGH
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- No specific ledger tension mapped; generic cost/benefit and tailoring tension still applies.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Track technical performance, margins, reserves and trends for decisions” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can teams report green current values while trends or margins make future failure likely remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- What decision threshold or action changes when the measure/risk/tailoring assessment changes?
- Can the metric or tailoring rule be gamed without improving system outcome?
