- **INTEGRATION_OBJECTIVE:** Reduces the probability, detection latency or consequence of: Teams report green current values while trends or margins make future failure likely. It should also improve the quality or speed of the named decision consumer; otherwise it should be retired or simplified.
- **SEQUENCE:** Order work by risk, dependency and learning only where composition is part of the failure mechanism.
- **ENTRY_IDENTITY:** Measure definition, data source, configuration, time and uncertainty must be stable enough for comparison.
- **ENVIRONMENT_OR_SURROGATE:** Measures and risk-retirement evidence need calibrated data, trend and uncertainty; scores alone are not evidence.
- **INTERFACE_TARGETS:** Shared budgets and cross-boundary risks require allocation and integration visibility.
- **ANOMALY_FLOW:** Feed relevant anomalies back to the governing source, decision or assumption.
- **REGRESSION:** Select after change impact and prior failure mechanisms.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Skip dashboards for unconstrained variables; track only measures that can change a decision.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A small, governed set of leading technical indicators with uncertainty and explicit response—not metric volume.

#### VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Track technical performance, margins, reserves and trends for decisions
- **CLAIM:** A specific requirement, architecture/interface proposition, model-use claim, mission/use claim or change-equivalence claim.
- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does it fulfil the current intended use/need in the relevant context?
- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Skip dashboards for unconstrained variables; track only measures that can change a decision.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A small, governed set of leading technical indicators with uncertainty and explicit response—not metric volume.

### P043 — Link technical risk and uncertainty to learning and decisions

- **FAILURE_MODE:** The register stays active while uncertainty remains untested and exposure silently grows.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Risk as an executable learning/contingency plan integrated with decisions and technical evidence.
- **TRIGGER:** Novel technology; uncertain environment; high consequence; architecture commitment; supplier dependency.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For a cheap uncertainty, run the direct experiment instead of scoring it repeatedly.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Thresholds, reserve policy, risk acceptance and corrective-action authority must be named.
- **CRITICISMS:** Ordinal risk matrices create false precision; opportunity language can dilute serious exposure; unknown unknowns remain.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Dashboards and risk registers are optional. The property is decision-bearing measurement, learning and contingency.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S073`, `S074`, `S087`, `S121`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S074`, `S073`, `S087`, `S121`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Authoritative objectives, measurable variables, causal understanding sufficient to select indicators and owners with authority to act.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Measures/risks must connect to mission or stakeholder outcomes and constraints.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Measure definition, data source, configuration, time and uncertainty must be stable enough for comparison.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Shared budgets and cross-boundary risks require allocation and integration visibility.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Measures and risk-retirement evidence need calibrated data, trend and uncertainty; scores alone are not evidence.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_TO_STRONG
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** HIGH
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- No specific ledger tension mapped; generic cost/benefit and tailoring tension still applies.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Link technical risk and uncertainty to learning and decisions” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can the register stays active while uncertainty remains untested and exposure silently grows remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- What decision threshold or action changes when the measure/risk/tailoring assessment changes?
- Can the metric or tailoring rule be gamed without improving system outcome?

### P044 — Tailor systems-engineering depth to consequence, complexity, uncertainty and reversibility

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Engineering effort is either wasted or underpowered relative to actual risk.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A trigger-based assurance architecture with explicit escalation and de-escalation, not project-size folklore or blanket waivers.
- **TRIGGER:** Every project; especially heterogeneous portfolios and mixed hardware/software.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Local reversible work should use direct evidence, short records and immediate feedback unless a trigger appears.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Tailoring decisions require named authority and cannot be self-exemption by the work producer alone where consequence is high.
- **CRITICISMS:** Tailoring can become arbitrary omission or negotiation by the most powerful team; standards offer little empirical calibration.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Tailoring matrices and plans are optional. The property is explicit activation/deactivation of engineering machinery by evidence-bearing triggers.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S001`, `S005`, `S009`, `S052`, `S118`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S001`, `S009`, `S118`, `S005`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** A transparent trigger model, authority to tailor, minimum non-waivable obligations and feedback on actual engineering outcomes.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Consequence and value are judged against current stakeholder/mission commitments.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Applicable lifecycle, product, change and evidence scope must be identified.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Coupling, organisational distribution and interface complexity are core escalation triggers.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Evidence fragility, consequence and reversibility determine review/V&V depth.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** WEAK_TO_MIXED
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** WEAK
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** HIGH
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Verification breadth versus cost and latency** — balancing properties: P037, P038, P040; hybrid: Claim- and change-specific V&V; risk-based sampling; layered evidence; staged rollout.
- **Traceability completeness versus maintenance burden** — balancing properties: P011, P028; hybrid: Consumer-defined relation types, automation with semantic checks, expiry and pruning.
- **Model detail versus usability/currentness** — balancing properties: P047, P049; hybrid: Layered views; minimum decision-sufficient fidelity; explicit authoritative scope; update-cost budget.
- **Configuration control versus rapid adaptation** — balancing properties: P027, P029; hybrid: Tiered authority; automated status/impact checks; emergency path; rollback and post-change reconciliation.
- **Architecture governance versus team autonomy** — balancing properties: P014, P017, P058; hybrid: Guardrails and interfaces centrally governed; local implementation delegated; exception path evidence-based.
- **Independent review versus rapid feedback** — balancing properties: P058; hybrid: Standing automated checks plus targeted independent challenge at high-risk claims/transitions.
- **Process depth versus small/reversible work** — balancing properties: P032, P051, P052; hybrid: Explicit activation thresholds, cheap path, progressive escalation and control retirement.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Tailor systems-engineering depth to consequence, complexity, uncertainty and reversibility” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can engineering effort is either wasted or underpowered relative to actual risk remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- What decision threshold or action changes when the measure/risk/tailoring assessment changes?
- Can the metric or tailoring rule be gamed without improving system outcome?

- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the applicable specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does the result remain fit for the current intended use when this property affects that judgement?
- **METHOD:** Evidence fragility, consequence and reversibility determine review/V&V depth.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Applicable lifecycle, product, change and evidence scope must be identified.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentive and claim opacity.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Evidence does not yield universal trigger thresholds or effort percentages.
- **ANOMALIES:** Blanket process; arbitrary waivers; size-only scaling; process accretion; inherited controls with no consumer; under-assurance under 'agile' labels.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Reconfirm after relevant source, configuration, interface, environment or use change.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Local reversible work should use direct evidence, short records and immediate feedback unless a trigger appears.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A trigger-based assurance architecture with explicit escalation and de-escalation, not project-size folklore or blanket waivers.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Tailor systems-engineering depth to consequence, complexity, uncertainty and reversibility
- **APPLICABILITY:** SUPPORTING_OR_NOT_PRIMARY_PROFILE: this property does not create an independent obligation in this profile; apply only where its stated trigger intersects the profile.
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Technical authority, project/product leadership, assurance authority and teams executing the cheap path.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Tailoring decisions require named authority and cannot be self-exemption by the work producer alone where consequence is high.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Evidence does not yield universal trigger thresholds or effort percentages.
- **PROVENANCE:** Lifecycle standards, defence pathway tailoring, agile SE and very-small-entity profiles.
- **PARITY:** Applicable lifecycle, product, change and evidence scope must be identified.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Coupling, organisational distribution and interface complexity are core escalation triggers.
- **VALIDATION:** Evidence fragility, consequence and reversibility determine review/V&V depth.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Signal when source, model, data, configuration or use becomes stale.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Local reversible work should use direct evidence, short records and immediate feedback unless a trigger appears.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A trigger-based assurance architecture with explicit escalation and de-escalation, not project-size folklore or blanket waivers.

</details>

### P045 — Engineer distributed authority and federated governance in systems of systems

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Global capability plans fail because dependencies cannot be compelled or synchronised.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Federated governance that states where authority ends and uses incentives/contracts/evidence rather than fictional central control.
- **TRIGGER:** Constituent systems have independent ownership, resources, goals and lifecycle.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Ordinary multi-team systems under one enforceable authority should use normal interface/configuration governance instead.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Central authority limits, constituent sovereignty, incentives, agreements and escalation must be explicit.
- **CRITICISMS:** Governance frameworks often lack enforceable authority and can become coordination forums without consequence.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** SoS boards and architecture frameworks are optional. The property is federated coordination under real independence and evolving composition.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S014`, `S089`, `S090`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S014`, `S089`, `S090`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Demonstrated operational and managerial independence, mission-level dependence, constituent identities, real authority map and communication mechanisms.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Global mission legitimacy and each constituent's local stakeholders/goals must both be represented.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Constituent versions, membership, compatibility and mission configurations change asynchronously and must be tracked.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Interoperability spans technical, semantic, organisational and policy interfaces.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Mission-thread and runtime/operational evidence must supplement constituent verification.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** WEAK_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** DOMAIN_BOUND
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** HIGH
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Interface standardisation versus local autonomy** — balancing properties: P023, P046; hybrid: Stable minimum contracts, version negotiation, adapters, compatibility windows and federated governance.
- **Systems-of-systems local authority versus global mission** — balancing properties: P005, P056; hybrid: Negotiated obligations, shared mission threads/measures, compatibility windows, escalation and visible unresolved gaps.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Engineer distributed authority and federated governance in systems of systems” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can global capability plans fail because dependencies cannot be compelled or synchronised remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Does any actor actually have authority over all constituents, or is federated governance required?
- How are asynchronous constituent changes and unresolved compatibility obligations surfaced?

### P046 — Manage interoperability and asynchronous evolution across independent constituents

- **FAILURE_MODE:** The SoS capability degrades through composition drift no single owner can see.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evolution-aware interoperability with graceful degradation and current evidence, not one-time standard adoption.

### P047 — Use model-centred representation only when it carries engineering decisions

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Model maintenance adds cost while real decisions continue in slides, spreadsheets and tacit knowledge.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Decision-driven modelling with explicit consumers, quality criteria, configuration and measured use; MBSE branding is irrelevant.
- **TRIGGER:** High relationship density; repeated analysis; multiple viewpoints; automation/interchange opportunity; long lifecycle.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For a simple project, structured text, tables, executable tests or sketches may be superior.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority is claim-specific; tool/repository ownership does not grant truth authority.
- **CRITICISMS:** Systematic reviews find claimed benefits often perceived, adoption costly and metrics immature.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** SysML, repositories, digital threads and twins are optional technologies. The property is decision-relevant, credible, current and portable structured knowledge.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S013`, `S018`, `S027`, `S053`, `S054`, `S098`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S053`, `S054`, `S098`, `S013`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Named model questions/consumers, governance, skilled users, versioning, tool/data access, validation and sustainable maintenance economics.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Models must represent authorised needs/use and disclose omitted stakeholders/context.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Model, metamodel, tool, data, transformation, implementation and physical referent versions must be related.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Semantic interoperability, transformation loss, APIs and supplier/tool boundaries must be tested.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Intended use, verification, validation, uncertainty, provenance, currentness and permissible inference are mandatory for consequential use.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG_AND_CURRENT
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** STRONG_FOR_REPRESENTED_CLAIMS
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** WEAK_TO_MIXED
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** WEAK
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** HIGH
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Model detail versus usability/currentness** — balancing properties: P044, P052; hybrid: Layered views; minimum decision-sufficient fidelity; explicit authoritative scope; update-cost budget.
- **Model automation versus analyst judgement** — balancing properties: P010, P041, P058; hybrid: Automation proposes/checks; humans own authority, assumptions, semantics and consequential acceptance; sampled audits.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Use model-centred representation only when it carries engineering decisions” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can model maintenance adds cost while real decisions continue in slides, spreadsheets and tacit knowledge remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Would another model, trace or digital representation materially change the next decision, or only increase model volume?
- Can a model be current but invalid for this use, or valid for the use but stale relative to the product?

### P048 — Federate models semantically and preserve representation portability

- **FAILURE_MODE:** A digital thread presents connected artefacts that are not semantically equivalent.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Plural specialised models connected by tested semantic mappings and portable identities—not forced monolithic modelling.
- **TRIGGER:** Multiple engineering disciplines/tools/suppliers; long-lived data; regulated handover; acquisition lock-in risk.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** One-team/one-tool short-lived work can use a stable open export and documented schema.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority is claim-specific; tool/repository ownership does not grant truth authority.
- **CRITICISMS:** Universal ontologies are costly and politically contested; standards lag tools; strict consistency can impede parallel work.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** SysML, repositories, digital threads and twins are optional technologies. The property is decision-relevant, credible, current and portable structured knowledge.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S021`, `S022`, `S078`, `S097`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S097`, `S078`, `S021`, `S022`

- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Semantic interoperability, transformation loss, APIs and supplier/tool boundaries must be tested.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Intended use, verification, validation, uncertainty, provenance, currentness and permissible inference are mandatory for consequential use.

### P049 — Bound digital-thread and digital-twin claims by provenance, currentness and validity

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Automation propagates stale, semantically mismatched or low-credibility information faster and with greater authority.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A provenance-rich, validity-bounded digital representation whose authority is claim- and use-specific; 'single source' means controlled reference, not metaphysical truth.
- **TRIGGER:** Cross-lifecycle reuse; operational twins; automated analysis; heterogeneous tools; high-consequence decisions.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where one immutable dataset and direct observation answer the question, avoid constructing a 'thread' or 'twin'.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority is claim-specific; tool/repository ownership does not grant truth authority.
- **CRITICISMS:** Definitions diverge, interoperability and validation standards remain incomplete, and vendor rhetoric often outruns measured benefit.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** SysML, repositories, digital threads and twins are optional technologies. The property is decision-relevant, credible, current and portable structured knowledge.

- **CURRENTNESS:** Re-establish after mission, authority, use-context, interface or configuration change.
- **VALIDATION:** Confirm that the governing stakeholder/mission claim remains legitimate and current enough for the decision.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where one immutable dataset and direct observation answer the question, avoid constructing a 'thread' or 'twin'.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A provenance-rich, validity-bounded digital representation whose authority is claim- and use-specific; 'single source' means controlled reference, not metaphysical truth.

#### REQUIREMENTS_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Bound digital-thread and digital-twin claims by provenance, currentness and validity
- **NEED_OR_SOURCE:** Must resolve to current stakeholder/mission evidence or an explicitly authorised constraint; for rejected candidates, this is the missing stronger relation.

- **PROVENANCE:** Source identity, version, rationale and derivation are required where the property can govern downstream work.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Material assumptions are explicit, owned and linked to falsification/expiry conditions.
- **AUTHORITY:** Authority is claim-specific; tool/repository ownership does not grant truth authority.
- **TRACEABILITY:** Only semantic relations needed by source, impact, implementation or evidence consumers; link presence alone is insufficient.
- **VALIDATION_PRECONDITION:** The governing need and intended use remain current enough to justify the requirement.
- **VERIFICATION_PRECONDITION:** The claim and applicable configuration are precise enough for a discriminating evidence method.
- **CHANGE_PROFILE:** Versioned; impacts and evidence are invalidated/reconfirmed after relevant source, assumption, architecture, interface or configuration change.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where one immutable dataset and direct observation answer the question, avoid constructing a 'thread' or 'twin'.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A provenance-rich, validity-bounded digital representation whose authority is claim- and use-specific; 'single source' means controlled reference, not metaphysical truth.

- **PROPERTY:** Bound digital-thread and digital-twin claims by provenance, currentness and validity
- **DRIVERS:** Current mission/stakeholder outcomes, hard constraints, quality attributes, interface/evolution risks and evidence gaps.
- **DECISION:** For every digital relation state source, transformation, configuration, update latency, uncertainty, intended use, validation evidence, trust/security and permissible inference.
- **ALTERNATIVES:** Materially different feasible options, including status quo/reference where informative; no token alternatives.
- **RATIONALE:** Decision context, criteria, evidence, uncertainty, rejected alternatives and consequences.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Owned, current, linked to the decision and given reopening/falsification triggers.
- **AUTHORITY:** Authority is claim-specific; tool/repository ownership does not grant truth authority.
- **CONFIGURATION:** Architecture decision and views/models identify the applicable product/model/configuration.
- **EVALUATION:** Scenario, analysis, prototype, model, review or integration evidence proportional to consequence.
- **REOPEN_TRIGGER:** Changed intent, invalidated assumption, margin erosion, interface/integration evidence, new alternative or configuration drift.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where one immutable dataset and direct observation answer the question, avoid constructing a 'thread' or 'twin'.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A provenance-rich, validity-bounded digital representation whose authority is claim- and use-specific; 'single source' means controlled reference, not metaphysical truth.

#### INTERFACE_ASSUMPTION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Bound digital-thread and digital-twin claims by provenance, currentness and validity
- **INTERFACE_SCOPE:** Physical, logical, data, timing, state/mode, protocol, organisational, authority and environmental dimensions as applicable.
- **OWNER:** Both endpoints plus joint resolution/escalation authority; a document custodian alone is insufficient.
- **SEMANTICS:** Units, ranges, meaning, timing, failure/degraded behaviour and provenance are explicit at risk-appropriate precision.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Cross-boundary assumptions are owned, current and testable/monitorable.
- **VERSION_IDENTITY:** Endpoint, contract, tool/data and environment versions are bound to evidence.
- **CHANGE_NOTIFICATION:** Change impact, compatibility window and revalidation responsibilities are explicit.
- **VERIFICATION:** Check each side's conformance to the contract.
- **INTEGRATION:** Exercise composition and end-to-end interactions in a credible context.
- **DEGRADATION:** Fallback, error handling and residual incompatibility are represented where consequential.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where one immutable dataset and direct observation answer the question, avoid constructing a 'thread' or 'twin'.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A provenance-rich, validity-bounded digital representation whose authority is claim- and use-specific; 'single source' means controlled reference, not metaphysical truth.

- **IDENTIFIED_ITEMS:** All representations/products/environments whose identity can change the claim or decision.
- **BASELINE_OR_CURRENT_STATE:** Authoritative current composition and supersession are explicit; a baseline is not presumed frozen forever.
- **CHANGE_AUTHORITY:** Authority is claim-specific; tool/repository ownership does not grant truth authority.
- **IMPACT_SCOPE:** Requirements, architecture, assumptions, interfaces, suppliers, models, code/hardware, tests, operations and claims as material.
- **STATUS_ACCOUNTING:** Decision, implementation, rollout, exception and actual operational state are distinguished.
- **EVIDENCE_CURRENTNESS:** Evidence cites configuration and has invalidation/reuse rules.
- **ROLLBACK_OR_RECOVERY:** Required where change consequence and reversibility justify it.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where one immutable dataset and direct observation answer the question, avoid constructing a 'thread' or 'twin'.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A provenance-rich, validity-bounded digital representation whose authority is claim- and use-specific; 'single source' means controlled reference, not metaphysical truth.

#### INTEGRATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Bound digital-thread and digital-twin claims by provenance, currentness and validity
- **INTEGRATION_OBJECTIVE:** Expose the highest-value composition uncertainty early enough to change design/action.
- **SEQUENCE:** Risk-, dependency- and learning-ordered rather than merely schedule-ordered.
- **ENTRY_IDENTITY:** Exact component/model/software/data/environment versions and readiness evidence.
- **ENVIRONMENT_OR_SURROGATE:** Fidelity, intended use, omissions and correlation are explicit.
- **INTERFACE_TARGETS:** Semantic, timing, state, physical, organisational and failure interactions.
- **ANOMALY_FLOW:** Observation, configuration, hypothesis, disposition, waiver and regression remain linked.
- **REGRESSION:** Selected from change impact and prior failure mechanisms.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where one immutable dataset and direct observation answer the question, avoid constructing a 'thread' or 'twin'.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A provenance-rich, validity-bounded digital representation whose authority is claim- and use-specific; 'single source' means controlled reference, not metaphysical truth.

#### VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Bound digital-thread and digital-twin claims by provenance, currentness and validity
- **CLAIM:** A specific requirement, architecture/interface proposition, model-use claim, mission/use claim or change-equivalence claim.
- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does it fulfil the current intended use/need in the relevant context?
- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where one immutable dataset and direct observation answer the question, avoid constructing a 'thread' or 'twin'.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A provenance-rich, validity-bounded digital representation whose authority is claim- and use-specific; 'single source' means controlled reference, not metaphysical truth.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Bound digital-thread and digital-twin claims by provenance, currentness and validity
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Named question, decision or evidence use; no value claim from existence or volume.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority is claim- and use-specific, never automatic truth.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Permissible decisions, assumptions, uncertainty, fidelity and caveats.
- **PROVENANCE:** Model/metamodel/tool/version, data and transformations are identified.
- **PARITY:** Implementation/physical/test/environment relations and permitted divergence are explicit.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Semantic mapping and transformation losses are tested; open export/API and exit path considered.
- **VALIDATION:** Credibility evidence proportionate to decision consequence.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Refresh latency, event triggers and stale-state signalling are explicit.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Where one immutable dataset and direct observation answer the question, avoid constructing a 'thread' or 'twin'.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A provenance-rich, validity-bounded digital representation whose authority is claim- and use-specific; 'single source' means controlled reference, not metaphysical truth.

### P050 — Reuse evidence only within explicit applicability boundaries

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Reuse reduces cost on paper while silently importing invalid assumptions.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence reuse as a bounded inference with explicit source identity, delta analysis and authority—not document inheritance.
- **TRIGGER:** Product families; software updates; supplier reuse; models; digital threads; certification-heavy domains.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Rerun a cheap decisive test rather than writing an elaborate similarity argument.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Technical evidence authority, acceptance authority and need/mission authority must not be conflated.
- **CRITICISMS:** Equivalence arguments can be circular or optimistic; excessive conservatism destroys reuse value.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Compliance matrices, test reports and V-model diagrams are optional. The property is bounded evidence for a specified claim and use.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S017`, `S044`, `S084`, `S112`, `S120`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S044`, `S112`, `S017`, `S084`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Explicit claims, authoritative baseline, fit-for-purpose environment/data, independent or competent evaluators and anomaly/limitation handling.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Validation claims require current intended use and representative stakeholders/operations.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Claim, item configuration, procedure/model/tool/data and environment must be fixed and recoverable.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** System and cross-boundary claims require composition evidence, not only component results.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** The property itself requires explicit evidence adequacy, coverage, uncertainty, residual limitations and currentness.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Evidence reuse versus stale-assumption risk** — balancing properties: P029, P038, P041; hybrid: Explicit applicability envelope, invalidation rules, delta analysis and revalidation.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Reuse evidence only within explicit applicability boundaries” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can reuse reduces cost on paper while silently importing invalid assumptions remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Would another model, trace or digital representation materially change the next decision, or only increase model volume?
- Can a model be current but invalid for this use, or valid for the use but stale relative to the product?

### P051 — Design rollback, recovery and change containment for consequential updates

- **FAILURE_MODE:** The system remains in a degraded or unsafe configuration while diagnosis continues.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Recovery evidence proportional to consequence, coupled to exact configuration and operational authority.
- **TRIGGER:** Frequent deployment; distributed operations; high availability; uncertain change impact; remote assets.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Cheap local reversible edits need only reliable versioning and a known-good state.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority to baseline, change, waive, release, roll back and supersede must be explicit and consequence-tiered.
- **CRITICISMS:** Rollback can be impossible for physical changes, database evolution, learned/adaptive state or external ecosystem effects; dual paths add complexity.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** CCBs, forms and baseline reviews are optional. The property is truthful identity, authority, implementation status and evidence continuity.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S081`, `S083`, `S087`, `S120`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S081`, `S083`, `S087`, `S120`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Governed items, stable identifiers, change authority, status accounting and access to actual product/model/test/environment state.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Configuration must be tied to the authorised capability, commitments and operational population.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** This family centrally requires exact item/composition/version/environment/evidence identity.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Interface and supplier configuration dependencies must be included in impact and release decisions.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Evidence must cite configuration and be invalidated or justified after change.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Configuration control versus rapid adaptation** — balancing properties: P027, P029; hybrid: Tiered authority; automated status/impact checks; emergency path; rollback and post-change reconciliation.
- **Process depth versus small/reversible work** — balancing properties: P044, P058; hybrid: Explicit activation thresholds, cheap path, progressive escalation and control retirement.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Design rollback, recovery and change containment for consequential updates” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can the system remains in a degraded or unsafe configuration while diagnosis continues remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Can the target recover safely from a consequential change and preserve evidence of the rollback/recovery state?
- Are stale controls retired with provenance, or do they remain falsely authoritative?

- **OWNER:** Both endpoints plus joint resolution/escalation authority; a document custodian alone is insufficient.
- **SEMANTICS:** Units, ranges, meaning, timing, failure/degraded behaviour and provenance are explicit at risk-appropriate precision.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Cross-boundary assumptions are owned, current and testable/monitorable.
- **VERSION_IDENTITY:** Endpoint, contract, tool/data and environment versions are bound to evidence.
- **CHANGE_NOTIFICATION:** Change impact, compatibility window and revalidation responsibilities are explicit.
- **VERIFICATION:** Check each side's conformance to the contract.
- **INTEGRATION:** Exercise composition and end-to-end interactions in a credible context.
- **DEGRADATION:** Fallback, error handling and residual incompatibility are represented where consequential.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Cheap local reversible edits need only reliable versioning and a known-good state.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Recovery evidence proportional to consequence, coupled to exact configuration and operational authority.

#### CONFIGURATION_CHANGE_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Design rollback, recovery and change containment for consequential updates
- **IDENTIFIED_ITEMS:** All representations/products/environments whose identity can change the claim or decision.
- **BASELINE_OR_CURRENT_STATE:** Authoritative current composition and supersession are explicit; a baseline is not presumed frozen forever.
- **CHANGE_AUTHORITY:** Authority to baseline, change, waive, release, roll back and supersede must be explicit and consequence-tiered.
- **IMPACT_SCOPE:** Requirements, architecture, assumptions, interfaces, suppliers, models, code/hardware, tests, operations and claims as material.
- **STATUS_ACCOUNTING:** Decision, implementation, rollout, exception and actual operational state are distinguished.
- **EVIDENCE_CURRENTNESS:** Evidence cites configuration and has invalidation/reuse rules.
- **ROLLBACK_OR_RECOVERY:** Required where change consequence and reversibility justify it.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Cheap local reversible edits need only reliable versioning and a known-good state.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Recovery evidence proportional to consequence, coupled to exact configuration and operational authority.

#### INTEGRATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Design rollback, recovery and change containment for consequential updates
- **INTEGRATION_OBJECTIVE:** Expose the highest-value composition uncertainty early enough to change design/action.
- **SEQUENCE:** Risk-, dependency- and learning-ordered rather than merely schedule-ordered.
- **ENTRY_IDENTITY:** Exact component/model/software/data/environment versions and readiness evidence.
- **ENVIRONMENT_OR_SURROGATE:** Fidelity, intended use, omissions and correlation are explicit.
- **INTERFACE_TARGETS:** Semantic, timing, state, physical, organisational and failure interactions.
- **ANOMALY_FLOW:** Observation, configuration, hypothesis, disposition, waiver and regression remain linked.
- **REGRESSION:** Selected from change impact and prior failure mechanisms.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Cheap local reversible edits need only reliable versioning and a known-good state.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Recovery evidence proportional to consequence, coupled to exact configuration and operational authority.

#### VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Design rollback, recovery and change containment for consequential updates
- **CLAIM:** A specific requirement, architecture/interface proposition, model-use claim, mission/use claim or change-equivalence claim.
- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does it fulfil the current intended use/need in the relevant context?
- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Cheap local reversible edits need only reliable versioning and a known-good state.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Recovery evidence proportional to consequence, coupled to exact configuration and operational authority.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Design rollback, recovery and change containment for consequential updates
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Named question, decision or evidence use; no value claim from existence or volume.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority is claim- and use-specific, never automatic truth.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Permissible decisions, assumptions, uncertainty, fidelity and caveats.
- **PROVENANCE:** Model/metamodel/tool/version, data and transformations are identified.
- **PARITY:** Implementation/physical/test/environment relations and permitted divergence are explicit.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Semantic mapping and transformation losses are tested; open export/API and exit path considered.
- **VALIDATION:** Credibility evidence proportionate to decision consequence.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Refresh latency, event triggers and stale-state signalling are explicit.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Cheap local reversible edits need only reliable versioning and a known-good state.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Recovery evidence proportional to consequence, coupled to exact configuration and operational authority.

</details>

### P052 — Retire stale requirements, models, reviews and controls without live consumers

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Teams cannot distinguish historical evidence from governing information, and process accretion becomes self-sustaining.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Lifecycle hygiene: preserve history while withdrawing governing status and maintenance obligations from information with no valid consumer.
- **TRIGGER:** Long-lived programmes; process layering; model proliferation; changing standards; post-certification evolution.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Delete or archive obviously unused low-risk artefacts immediately under ordinary version control.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Tailoring decisions require named authority and cannot be self-exemption by the work producer alone where consequence is high.
- **CRITICISMS:** Premature retirement can erase future evidence or organisational memory; consumers may be latent or regulatory.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Tailoring matrices and plans are optional. The property is explicit activation/deactivation of engineering machinery by evidence-bearing triggers.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S058`, `S069`, `S071`, `S098`, `S100`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S069`, `S071`, `S098`, `S058`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** A transparent trigger model, authority to tailor, minimum non-waivable obligations and feedback on actual engineering outcomes.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Consequence and value are judged against current stakeholder/mission commitments.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Applicable lifecycle, product, change and evidence scope must be identified.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Coupling, organisational distribution and interface complexity are core escalation triggers.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Evidence fragility, consequence and reversibility determine review/V&V depth.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** WEAK_TO_MIXED
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** WEAK
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** HIGH
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Traceability completeness versus maintenance burden** — balancing properties: P011, P028; hybrid: Consumer-defined relation types, automation with semantic checks, expiry and pruning.
- **Model detail versus usability/currentness** — balancing properties: P047, P049; hybrid: Layered views; minimum decision-sufficient fidelity; explicit authoritative scope; update-cost budget.
- **Process depth versus small/reversible work** — balancing properties: P044, P058; hybrid: Explicit activation thresholds, cheap path, progressive escalation and control retirement.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Retire stale requirements, models, reviews and controls without live consumers” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can teams cannot distinguish historical evidence from governing information, and process accretion becomes self-sustaining remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Can the target recover safely from a consequential change and preserve evidence of the rollback/recovery state?
- Are stale controls retired with provenance, or do they remain falsely authoritative?

### P053 — Include enabling systems and lifecycle stakeholders in system definition

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Local product optimisation shifts cost and failure into later lifecycle stages or enabling systems.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Selective whole-lifecycle inclusion driven by consequence and dependency, not exhaustive lifecycle documentation.
- **TRIGGER:** Long-lived physical systems; specialised operations; maintainability/logistics; certification; hazardous disposal.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For short-lived software/services using standard infrastructure, capture only enabling dependencies that can alter feasibility or operation.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority across lifecycle organisations and product-family/mission boundaries must be explicit.
- **CRITICISMS:** Lifecycle scope can expand without bound; future support/disposal assumptions are uncertain.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Lifecycle plans, HSI plans and mission-thread artefacts are optional. The property is early inclusion of dependencies that materially change system decisions.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S001`, `S003`, `S037`, `S038`, `S111`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S001`, `S037`, `S003`, `S038`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Defined lifecycle/system boundary, representative lifecycle stakeholders, dependency/economic rationale and configuration/feedback mechanisms.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Operators, maintainers, manufacturers, support, disposal, regulators and portfolio/mission actors are included only where materially affected.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Product variants, enabling systems, operational populations and lifecycle states must be identifiable.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Enabling-system, human, platform and mission dependencies are treated as interfaces/compositions.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Operational, maintainability, variability and mission evidence must match the claimed lifecycle scope.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- No specific ledger tension mapped; generic cost/benefit and tailoring tension still applies.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Include enabling systems and lifecycle stakeholders in system definition” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can local product optimisation shifts cost and failure into later lifecycle stages or enabling systems remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Are operators, maintainers, enabling systems, human roles and cross-system mission actors represented where they change engineering?
- Is this genuinely a lifecycle/mission/SoS trigger, or a domain label being applied to ordinary local work?

- **CHEAP_PATH:** For short-lived software/services using standard infrastructure, capture only enabling dependencies that can alter feasibility or operation.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Selective whole-lifecycle inclusion driven by consequence and dependency, not exhaustive lifecycle documentation.

#### VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Include enabling systems and lifecycle stakeholders in system definition
- **CLAIM:** A specific requirement, architecture/interface proposition, model-use claim, mission/use claim or change-equivalence claim.
- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does it fulfil the current intended use/need in the relevant context?
- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For short-lived software/services using standard infrastructure, capture only enabling dependencies that can alter feasibility or operation.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Selective whole-lifecycle inclusion driven by consequence and dependency, not exhaustive lifecycle documentation.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Include enabling systems and lifecycle stakeholders in system definition
- **APPLICABILITY:** SUPPORTING_OR_NOT_PRIMARY_PROFILE: this property does not create an independent obligation in this profile; apply only where its stated trigger intersects the profile.
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Product/mission owner, architect, operations/support, platform governance and lifecycle authorities.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority across lifecycle organisations and product-family/mission boundaries must be explicit.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Benefits are difficult to isolate from programme management and logistics practices.
- **PROVENANCE:** Lifecycle SE and concurrent engineering.
- **PARITY:** Product variants, enabling systems, operational populations and lifecycle states must be identifiable.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Enabling-system, human, platform and mission dependencies are treated as interfaces/compositions.
- **VALIDATION:** Operational, maintainability, variability and mission evidence must match the claimed lifecycle scope.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Signal when source, model, data, configuration or use becomes stale.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For short-lived software/services using standard infrastructure, capture only enabling dependencies that can alter feasibility or operation.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Selective whole-lifecycle inclusion driven by consequence and dependency, not exhaustive lifecycle documentation.

</details>

### P054 — Allocate functions and authority across hardware, software, humans and organisations

- **FAILURE_MODE:** The technical system passes but operators cannot control, understand, maintain or safely recover it.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Evidence-based joint human–technical allocation linked to operational validation and change, not a generic 'human factors' checklist.
- **TRIGGER:** Human-in/on-the-loop systems; autonomy; complex maintenance; high workload; organisational handoffs.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Simple tools with direct user control may validate allocation through ordinary use tests.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority across lifecycle organisations and product-family/mission boundaries must be explicit.
- **CRITICISMS:** This property shares ancestry with human factors; detailed cognitive-fit methods lie outside this lane.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Lifecycle plans, HSI plans and mission-thread artefacts are optional. The property is early inclusion of dependencies that materially change system decisions.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S085`, `S111`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S085`, `S111`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Defined lifecycle/system boundary, representative lifecycle stakeholders, dependency/economic rationale and configuration/feedback mechanisms.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Operators, maintainers, manufacturers, support, disposal, regulators and portfolio/mission actors are included only where materially affected.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Product variants, enabling systems, operational populations and lifecycle states must be identifiable.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Enabling-system, human, platform and mission dependencies are treated as interfaces/compositions.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Operational, maintainability, variability and mission evidence must match the claimed lifecycle scope.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- No specific ledger tension mapped; generic cost/benefit and tailoring tension still applies.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Allocate functions and authority across hardware, software, humans and organisations” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can the technical system passes but operators cannot control, understand, maintain or safely recover it remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Are operators, maintainers, enabling systems, human roles and cross-system mission actors represented where they change engineering?
- Is this genuinely a lifecycle/mission/SoS trigger, or a domain label being applied to ordinary local work?

### P055 — Govern platform, product-line and variability architecture

- **FAILURE_MODE:** One member's change silently breaks another or invalidates family-wide claims.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Configuration- and evidence-aware variability management with explicit commonality trade-offs.
- **TRIGGER:** Multiple related products/configurations; long-term reuse; configurable systems; platform ecosystems.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** A one-off product or two loosely related variants should not trigger a product-line infrastructure.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority across lifecycle organisations and product-family/mission boundaries must be explicit.
- **CRITICISMS:** Combinatorial explosion makes exhaustive testing impossible; platform governance can constrain innovation and optimise yesterday's variants.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Lifecycle plans, HSI plans and mission-thread artefacts are optional. The property is early inclusion of dependencies that materially change system decisions.

- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Operational, maintainability, variability and mission evidence must match the claimed lifecycle scope.

### P056 — Engineer mission/capability relationships beyond a single system when the mission is the unit of value

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Delivered systems meet local requirements but do not create the intended end-to-end capability.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A mission-level extension activated by genuine cross-system capability dependence, not relabelling every project as a mission.
- **TRIGGER:** Defence/civil capability portfolios; mission outcomes span independently managed systems.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Ordinary products whose value is directly observable at the product boundary do not require mission-engineering machinery.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority across lifecycle organisations and product-family/mission boundaries must be explicit.
- **CRITICISMS:** Defence-heavy lineage, uncertain authority and potential duplication of SoS/portfolio management; outcome evidence limited.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Lifecycle plans, HSI plans and mission-thread artefacts are optional. The property is early inclusion of dependencies that materially change system decisions.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S089`, `S114`, `S115`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S089`, `S115`, `S114`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Defined lifecycle/system boundary, representative lifecycle stakeholders, dependency/economic rationale and configuration/feedback mechanisms.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Operators, maintainers, manufacturers, support, disposal, regulators and portfolio/mission actors are included only where materially affected.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Product variants, enabling systems, operational populations and lifecycle states must be identifiable.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Enabling-system, human, platform and mission dependencies are treated as interfaces/compositions.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Operational, maintainability, variability and mission evidence must match the claimed lifecycle scope.

- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** LOW_OUTSIDE_TRIGGER_DOMAIN
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Systems-of-systems local authority versus global mission** — balancing properties: P045, P046; hybrid: Negotiated obligations, shared mission threads/measures, compatibility windows, escalation and visible unresolved gaps.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Engineer mission/capability relationships beyond a single system when the mission is the unit of value” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can delivered systems meet local requirements but do not create the intended end-to-end capability remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Are operators, maintainers, enabling systems, human roles and cross-system mission actors represented where they change engineering?
- Is this genuinely a lifecycle/mission/SoS trigger, or a domain label being applied to ordinary local work?

### P057 — Use AI-assisted requirements, architecture and modelling under human, provenance and validation controls

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Automation scales hallucination, bias and false coherence into an authoritative engineering baseline.
- **MATURE_FORM:** AI as a fallible hypothesis and retrieval/translation aid inside governed SE, never an autonomous authority for need, architecture or assurance.
- **TRIGGER:** High-volume drafting/search; candidate generation; trace recovery; model queries; bounded low-consequence assistance.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Do not use AI where a deterministic parser/check/query is cheaper and more reliable, or where outputs cannot be adequately validated.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority is claim-specific; tool/repository ownership does not grant truth authority.
- **CRITICISMS:** Evidence is predominantly early-stage, laboratory and rapidly obsolete; reproducibility, interpretability, hallucination and governance are unresolved.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** SysML, repositories, digital threads and twins are optional technologies. The property is decision-relevant, credible, current and portable structured knowledge.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S101`, `S102`, `S103`, `S104`, `S105`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S101`, `S102`, `S103`, `S104`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Named model questions/consumers, governance, skilled users, versioning, tool/data access, validation and sustainable maintenance economics.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Models must represent authorised needs/use and disclose omitted stakeholders/context.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Model, metamodel, tool, data, transformation, implementation and physical referent versions must be related.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Semantic interoperability, transformation loss, APIs and supplier/tool boundaries must be tested.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Intended use, verification, validation, uncertainty, provenance, currentness and permissible inference are mandatory for consequential use.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG_AND_CURRENT
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** STRONG_FOR_REPRESENTED_CLAIMS
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** WEAK_AND_FAST_MOVING
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** WEAK
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** HIGH
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Model automation versus analyst judgement** — balancing properties: P010, P041, P058; hybrid: Automation proposes/checks; humans own authority, assumptions, semantics and consequential acceptance; sampled audits.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Use AI-assisted requirements, architecture and modelling under human, provenance and validation controls” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can automation scales hallucination, bias and false coherence into an authoritative engineering baseline remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Are AI outputs treated as proposed, provenance-bearing claims rather than authoritative engineering truth?
- What independent semantic and technical validation is required before consequential use?

### P058 — Apply independent technical challenge where consequence and incentive justify it

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Contradictory evidence, optimism and local blind spots survive transition.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Consequence-proportionate independent challenge with real information and decision effect; no presumption that more boards mean more assurance.
- **TRIGGER:** High consequence; irreversible commitment; novel architecture; conflicted suppliers; disputed anomalies.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** Low-risk reversible work can use peer pairing, automated checks and direct feedback rather than a standing board.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Reviewer independence and authority to block/escalate/record dissent must be real.
- **CRITICISMS:** GAO documented review bodies that existed but were ineffective; reviews add delay and can diffuse accountability.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Boards, panels and IV&V organisations are optional implementations. The property is consequential, competent and sufficiently independent challenge.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S046`, `S047`, `S106`, `S107`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S046`, `S047`, `S106`, `S107`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Material claim/decision, identifiable incentives and expertise, access to evidence, dissent path, decision authority and proportionality.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Challenge scope must include the stakeholders/mission harmed by false confidence.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Reviewed claim, baseline, evidence and disposition must be fixed and versioned.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Cross-boundary decisions require representation from affected interfaces without letting consensus replace challenge.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Review must inspect evidence adequacy and limitations, not merely work-product presence.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** HIGH
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Architecture governance versus team autonomy** — balancing properties: P021, P044; hybrid: Guardrails and interfaces centrally governed; local implementation delegated; exception path evidence-based.
- **Independent review versus rapid feedback** — balancing properties: P032, P044; hybrid: Standing automated checks plus targeted independent challenge at high-risk claims/transitions.
- **Model automation versus analyst judgement** — balancing properties: P047, P057; hybrid: Automation proposes/checks; humans own authority, assumptions, semantics and consequential acceptance; sampled audits.
- **Process depth versus small/reversible work** — balancing properties: P032, P051, P052; hybrid: Explicit activation thresholds, cheap path, progressive escalation and control retirement.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Apply independent technical challenge where consequence and incentive justify it” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can contradictory evidence, optimism and local blind spots survive transition remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Is independent challenge targeted where producer incentives, opacity or consequence warrant it?
- Can the reviewer stop or change the decision, and is dissent preserved?

#### STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PROFILE
