### P059 — Feed integration and operational evidence back into intent, requirements and architecture

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Teams fix symptoms while preserving the false premise that generated them.
- **MATURE_FORM:** A closed learning loop from composition/use to governing intent and decisions, with transparent rebaselining.
- **TRIGGER:** Novel systems; progressive requirements; operational pilots; emergent interactions; changed use.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** A straightforward implementation defect with unchanged premise can be fixed locally.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Someone must control integration configuration, sequence, facilities, discrepancy disposition and stop/go decisions.
- **CRITICISMS:** Upstream reopening can destabilise programmes and be abused to avoid accountability; contractual authority may resist learning.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** Integration plans and readiness reviews are optional carriers. The property is early enough, risk-ranked composition evidence.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S042`, `S049`, `S085`, `S108`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S042`, `S049`, `S108`, `S003`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Defined interfaces/configurations, integration authority, suitable environments, entry evidence, anomaly process and feasible sequencing.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration must exercise the mission/use interactions that matter, not only convenient assembly paths.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** Every integration result must identify products, software/data, environment, tools and configuration.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Risky semantic, timing, physical, organisational and state interfaces are first-class targets.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Integration evidence is not automatically verification or validation; claims, coverage and limitations must be stated.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Upfront architecture versus evolutionary learning** — balancing properties: P013, P014, P017; hybrid: Set-based alternatives; minimum architecture runway; explicit decision-reopening triggers; incremental integration.
- **Stable baselines versus changing intent** — balancing properties: P026, P027; hybrid: Time-bounded/incremental baselines with named authority, impact propagation and revalidation.
- **Requirements completeness versus discovery** — balancing properties: P006, P008, P012; hybrid: Progressive elaboration; assumptions; scenarios; unknown/TBD inventory; risk-ordered refinement.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Feed integration and operational evidence back into intent, requirements and architecture” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can teams fix symptoms while preserving the false premise that generated them remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Does new integration/operational evidence actually revise upstream intent, requirements and architecture?
- Can future engineers recover the current decision and evidence without reconstructing it from scattered artefacts?

### P060 — Maintain accessible, current technical data and decision records

- **FAILURE_MODE:** Decisions are repeated, suppliers cannot integrate, maintainers cannot diagnose and reviewers rely on presentation summaries.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Technical knowledge continuity as governed access and identity, not accumulation or one-tool centralisation.
- **TRIGGER:** Long lifecycle; supplier turnover; regulated evidence; multi-tool environment; sustainment.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For small work, a well-structured repository with immutable history and clear current pointers may be enough.
- **AUTHORITY_BOUNDARY:** Authority to baseline, change, waive, release, roll back and supersede must be explicit and consequence-tiered.
- **CRITICISMS:** Central repositories create lock-in and stale duplication; access control can block the very cross-boundary use intended.
- **ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY:** CCBs, forms and baseline reviews are optional. The property is truthful identity, authority, implementation status and evidence continuity.
- **PRIMARY_SOURCE_IDS:** `S003`, `S068`, `S094`, `S116`
- **CRITICAL_SOURCE_IDS:** `S094`, `S068`, `S003`, `S116`

#### REQUIRED_PRECONDITIONS
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Governed items, stable identifiers, change authority, status accounting and access to actual product/model/test/environment state.
- **STAKEHOLDER_OR_MISSION_PRECONDITIONS:** Configuration must be tied to the authorised capability, commitments and operational population.
- **CONFIGURATION_OR_IDENTITY_PRECONDITIONS:** This family centrally requires exact item/composition/version/environment/evidence identity.
- **INTERFACE_OR_INTEGRATION_PRECONDITIONS:** Interface and supplier configuration dependencies must be included in impact and release decisions.
- **EVIDENCE_OR_VV_PRECONDITIONS:** Evidence must cite configuration and be invalidated or justified after change.

#### EVIDENCE_STRENGTH
- **HISTORICAL_PROVENANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **STANDARD_OR_GUIDANCE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FORMAL_OR_MODEL_STRENGTH:** MODERATE
- **EMPIRICAL_COMPARATIVE_STRENGTH:** LIMITED_TO_MODERATE
- **PROJECT_OR_PROGRAMME_CASE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **FIELD_PRACTICE_STRENGTH:** STRONG
- **REPLICATION_OR_MULTI_STUDY_STRENGTH:** LIMITED
- **TRANSFERABILITY_STRENGTH:** STRONG_WITH_TRIGGER_AND_CHEAP_PATH
- **ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVITY:** MODERATE
- **CONTRARY_EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE

#### POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY
- **Single authoritative representation versus plural specialised models** — balancing properties: P048; hybrid: Authority by claim; federated models; mappings and reconciliation; loss disclosure.
- **Digital continuity versus tool lock-in** — balancing properties: P048; hybrid: Open APIs/export, canonical interchange, provenance, exit tests and modular integration.

#### QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT
- Does the target system implement “Maintain accessible, current technical data and decision records” when its stated trigger is present, or does it only produce a similarly named artefact?
- Can decisions are repeated, suppliers cannot integrate, maintainers cannot diagnose and reviewers rely on presentation summaries remain apparently green after the governing source, assumption or configuration changes?
- Who has authority to create, change, challenge and retire the governing claim, and is that authority distinct from technical truth?
- What real engineering decision, integration action or evidence judgement consumes this property?
- Is the selected implementation the cheapest discriminator/control that can address the failure mode, or is a lower-cost path available?
- Are configuration identity, interface assumptions, currentness and residual limitations preserved strongly enough to support the claimed result?
- Does new integration/operational evidence actually revise upstream intent, requirements and architecture?
- Can future engineers recover the current decision and evidence without reconstructing it from scattered artefacts?

#### REQUIREMENTS_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Maintain accessible, current technical data and decision records
- **NEED_OR_SOURCE:** Must resolve to current stakeholder/mission evidence or an explicitly authorised constraint; for rejected candidates, this is the missing stronger relation.
- **REQUIREMENT_FORM:** Structured text, model, contract, scenario, constraint, testable property or other fit-for-purpose representation; no mandatory template.
- **PROVENANCE:** Source identity, version, rationale and derivation are required where the property can govern downstream work.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Material assumptions are explicit, owned and linked to falsification/expiry conditions.

- **MATURE_FORM:** Technical knowledge continuity as governed access and identity, not accumulation or one-tool centralisation.

#### ARCHITECTURE_DECISION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Maintain accessible, current technical data and decision records
- **DRIVERS:** Current mission/stakeholder outcomes, hard constraints, quality attributes, interface/evolution risks and evidence gaps.
- **DECISION:** Identify authoritative records by claim; preserve provenance, access, portability, retention, version and consumer; distinguish governing, working and historical information.
- **ALTERNATIVES:** Materially different feasible options, including status quo/reference where informative; no token alternatives.
- **RATIONALE:** Decision context, criteria, evidence, uncertainty, rejected alternatives and consequences.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Owned, current, linked to the decision and given reopening/falsification triggers.
- **AUTHORITY:** Authority to baseline, change, waive, release, roll back and supersede must be explicit and consequence-tiered.
- **CONFIGURATION:** Architecture decision and views/models identify the applicable product/model/configuration.
- **EVALUATION:** Scenario, analysis, prototype, model, review or integration evidence proportional to consequence.
- **REOPEN_TRIGGER:** Changed intent, invalidated assumption, margin erosion, interface/integration evidence, new alternative or configuration drift.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For small work, a well-structured repository with immutable history and clear current pointers may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Technical knowledge continuity as governed access and identity, not accumulation or one-tool centralisation.

#### INTERFACE_ASSUMPTION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Maintain accessible, current technical data and decision records
- **INTERFACE_SCOPE:** Physical, logical, data, timing, state/mode, protocol, organisational, authority and environmental dimensions as applicable.
- **OWNER:** Both endpoints plus joint resolution/escalation authority; a document custodian alone is insufficient.
- **SEMANTICS:** Units, ranges, meaning, timing, failure/degraded behaviour and provenance are explicit at risk-appropriate precision.
- **ASSUMPTIONS:** Cross-boundary assumptions are owned, current and testable/monitorable.
- **VERSION_IDENTITY:** Endpoint, contract, tool/data and environment versions are bound to evidence.
- **CHANGE_NOTIFICATION:** Change impact, compatibility window and revalidation responsibilities are explicit.
- **VERIFICATION:** Check each side's conformance to the contract.
- **INTEGRATION:** Exercise composition and end-to-end interactions in a credible context.
- **DEGRADATION:** Fallback, error handling and residual incompatibility are represented where consequential.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For small work, a well-structured repository with immutable history and clear current pointers may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Technical knowledge continuity as governed access and identity, not accumulation or one-tool centralisation.

#### CONFIGURATION_CHANGE_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Maintain accessible, current technical data and decision records
- **IDENTIFIED_ITEMS:** All representations/products/environments whose identity can change the claim or decision.
- **BASELINE_OR_CURRENT_STATE:** Authoritative current composition and supersession are explicit; a baseline is not presumed frozen forever.
- **CHANGE_AUTHORITY:** Authority to baseline, change, waive, release, roll back and supersede must be explicit and consequence-tiered.
- **IMPACT_SCOPE:** Requirements, architecture, assumptions, interfaces, suppliers, models, code/hardware, tests, operations and claims as material.
- **STATUS_ACCOUNTING:** Decision, implementation, rollout, exception and actual operational state are distinguished.
- **EVIDENCE_CURRENTNESS:** Evidence cites configuration and has invalidation/reuse rules.
- **ROLLBACK_OR_RECOVERY:** Required where change consequence and reversibility justify it.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For small work, a well-structured repository with immutable history and clear current pointers may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Technical knowledge continuity as governed access and identity, not accumulation or one-tool centralisation.

#### INTEGRATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Maintain accessible, current technical data and decision records
- **INTEGRATION_OBJECTIVE:** Expose the highest-value composition uncertainty early enough to change design/action.
- **SEQUENCE:** Risk-, dependency- and learning-ordered rather than merely schedule-ordered.
- **ENTRY_IDENTITY:** Exact component/model/software/data/environment versions and readiness evidence.
- **ENVIRONMENT_OR_SURROGATE:** Fidelity, intended use, omissions and correlation are explicit.
- **INTERFACE_TARGETS:** Semantic, timing, state, physical, organisational and failure interactions.
- **ANOMALY_FLOW:** Observation, configuration, hypothesis, disposition, waiver and regression remain linked.
- **REGRESSION:** Selected from change impact and prior failure mechanisms.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For small work, a well-structured repository with immutable history and clear current pointers may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Technical knowledge continuity as governed access and identity, not accumulation or one-tool centralisation.

#### VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Maintain accessible, current technical data and decision records
- **CLAIM:** A specific requirement, architecture/interface proposition, model-use claim, mission/use claim or change-equivalence claim.
- **VERIFICATION_QUESTION:** Does the identified configuration satisfy the specified claim?
- **VALIDATION_QUESTION:** Does it fulfil the current intended use/need in the relevant context?
- **METHOD:** Test, analysis, inspection, demonstration, simulation, operational evidence or combination selected for discrimination and cost.
- **ENVIRONMENT_AND_DATA:** Representative enough for the claim; provenance, fidelity and limitations explicit.
- **INDEPENDENCE:** Proportionate to consequence, producer incentives and claim uncertainty.
- **COVERAGE_AND_LIMITS:** Coverage is stated but never equated with absence of unknown behaviour.
- **ANOMALIES:** Contradictory and unresolved evidence remains visible.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Bound to configuration, assumptions, intended use and change impact.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For small work, a well-structured repository with immutable history and clear current pointers may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Technical knowledge continuity as governed access and identity, not accumulation or one-tool centralisation.

#### MODEL_OR_DIGITAL_PROFILE
- **PROPERTY:** Maintain accessible, current technical data and decision records
- **MODEL_OR_DATA_PURPOSE:** Named question, decision or evidence use; no value claim from existence or volume.
- **AUTHORITATIVE_SCOPE:** Authority is claim- and use-specific, never automatic truth.
- **INTENDED_USE_AND_LIMITS:** Permissible decisions, assumptions, uncertainty, fidelity and caveats.
- **PROVENANCE:** Model/metamodel/tool/version, data and transformations are identified.
- **PARITY:** Implementation/physical/test/environment relations and permitted divergence are explicit.
- **INTEROPERABILITY:** Semantic mapping and transformation losses are tested; open export/API and exit path considered.
- **VALIDATION:** Credibility evidence proportionate to decision consequence.
- **CURRENTNESS:** Refresh latency, event triggers and stale-state signalling are explicit.
- **CHEAP_PATH:** For small work, a well-structured repository with immutable history and clear current pointers may be enough.
- **MATURE_FORM:** Technical knowledge continuity as governed access and identity, not accumulation or one-tool centralisation.

</details>

## CEREMONIES_TO_NOT_BLINDLY_ADOPT

- A universal one-pass V-model or fixed waterfall lifecycle
- Exhaustive frozen upfront requirements
- Maximal traceability/link-count targets
- Mandatory SysML, monolithic system model, digital thread or digital twin
- Architecture diagrams without decisions/rationale
- Signed ICDs without semantic/current integration evidence
- Acceptance sign-off as technical truth
- Standing review boards and gates without decision information gain
- Weighted-score matrices presented as objective truth
- A literal single source of truth
- Model-volume/detail metrics
- Standards/process compliance as system coherence or ROI

## CONTEXTS_WHERE_PROPERTY_SHOULD_NOT_TRIGGER

- Low-consequence, local, reversible work with direct observability and cheap rollback should not trigger heavyweight boards, full lifecycle documentation or exhaustive trace.
- A named artefact should not trigger when no live engineering, authority, change, integration or evidence consumer exists.
- Systems-of-systems governance should not trigger where one authority controls constituent systems and lifecycle.
- Digital thread/twin or formal MBSE should not trigger when a narrower record, test or analysis settles the decision.
- Independent V&V should not trigger uniformly where producer evidence is transparent, consequence is low and rapid feedback/rollback controls exposure.
- Formal trade scoring should not trigger when constraints, dominance or one cheap experiment discriminates alternatives.
- Formal baselines/change boards should not trigger for isolated changes whose affected state and evidence are automatically identifiable and reversible.
- Product-line/variability machinery should not trigger for a one-off system with no planned reuse/variation.
- Mission-engineering machinery should not trigger when value and authority are contained within one system.
- Retired or stale controls should not continue merely because they were once mandated; legal/contractual retention is a separate constraint.

## PROPERTIES_REQUIRING_INDEPENDENT_VV

- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P038, P039, P040, P041, P058; **BOUNDARY:** Independence is proportional to consequence, claim opacity, producer incentives, novelty and public/contractual trust; it is not universal standing-board ceremony.

## MODEL_BASED_PROPERTIES_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMITS

- **PROPERTY_ID:** P030; **LIMIT:** Parity can be specified and checked, but no repository can guarantee that every relevant physical/operational state is represented.
- **PROPERTY_ID:** P041; **LIMIT:** Credibility is intended-use-specific and cannot be automatically transferred.
- **PROPERTY_ID:** P047; **LIMIT:** Measured MBSE benefits remain sparse; decision/task utility must be demonstrated locally.
- **PROPERTY_ID:** P048; **LIMIT:** Interchange/API support does not eliminate semantic loss, viewpoint mismatch or tool dependence.
- **PROPERTY_ID:** P049; **LIMIT:** Digital continuity/currentness does not establish real-world correctness or trust.
- **PROPERTY_ID:** P050; **LIMIT:** Evidence reuse is only as sound as the applicability/invalidity analysis.
- **PROPERTY_ID:** P057; **LIMIT:** AI assistance does not inherit authority, correctness or reproducibility.

## SYSTEMS_OF_SYSTEMS_ONLY_PROPERTIES

- **PROPERTY_ID:** P045; **BOUNDARY:** Triggers only with genuine distributed operational/managerial authority.
- **PROPERTY_ID:** P046; **BOUNDARY:** Triggers with independently evolving constituents and negotiated interoperability.
- **PROPERTY_ID:** P056; **BOUNDARY:** Mission/capability engineering is domain-specific and may overlap SoS only when the mission crosses autonomous constituents.

## DOMAIN_CERTIFICATION_OR_ACQUISITION_SPECIFIC_PROPERTIES

- Formal qualification/acceptance authorities and prescribed review artefacts
- Defence mission-engineering governance and acquisition milestone structures
- Space-specific model/simulation credibility and verification standards
- Technology readiness formalism and mandated independent assessment
- Contractual configuration/data deliverables and supplier audit forms

## PROPERTIES_WITH_STRONG_EMPIRICAL_OR_PROGRAMME_SUPPORT

- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P006, P011; **SUPPORT:** Controlled and field evidence supports maintained traceability for selected evolution/maintenance tasks, bounded by cost and semantic quality.; **SOURCES:** S057, S058
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P023, P024, P025; **SUPPORT:** Mishap and programme lessons repeatedly expose unit, semantic, documentation and integration-interface failures.; **SOURCES:** S043, S079
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P019, P031, P032, P037, P042, P043; **SUPPORT:** GAO multi-programme evidence supports acquiring design/manufacturing knowledge, technology maturity and integration evidence before irreversible commitment.; **SOURCES:** S047, S048, S049, S050
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P026, P028, P029, P030; **SUPPORT:** Configuration/change lineages and mishap evidence support exact identity and propagation/currentness duties.; **SOURCES:** S034, S035, S036, S043, S080, S081, S082
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P036, P038, P039, P040, P041; **SUPPORT:** Standards, handbooks and mishaps strongly support distinct claim, configuration, method, intended-use and model-credibility boundaries.; **SOURCES:** S003, S015, S016, S017, S084, S085, S086

## PROPERTIES_WITH_MIXED_OR_WEAK_SUPPORT

- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P005; **REASON:** Outcome measures are necessary but susceptible to proxy gaming; optimal measure systems are domain-specific.
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P008; **REASON:** Quality attributes/smells are useful screens; empirical definitions and outcome links remain uneven.
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P016, P018; **REASON:** Modularity/debt concepts are plausible and field-supported, but system-level comparative measures are immature.
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P020; **REASON:** Sensitivity is formally justified; practical multi-criteria governance remains gameable.
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P044; **REASON:** Tailoring is strongly guided but quantitative trigger/ROI evidence is limited.
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P047, P048, P049; **REASON:** MBSE/digital benefits are case- and organisation-dependent, with sparse measured comparison.
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P055; **REASON:** Product-line benefits depend heavily on domain, reuse horizon and variability stability.
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P057; **REASON:** AI-assisted SE is rapidly changing and lacks stable assurance/replication boundaries.
- **PROPERTY_IDS:** P058; **REASON:** Independent challenge is compelling for high consequence, but the marginal value of review structures is poorly quantified.

## UNRESOLVED_PROPERTIES

- **PROPERTY_ID:** P057; **STATUS:** CONTESTED; **QUESTION:** Which AI-assisted SE tasks can achieve replicated net benefit while preserving authority, provenance, confidentiality, semantic correctness and V&V?

## Audit-use boundary

This intake does not establish ownership, deficiency, redundancy, incompatibility or adoption for any target system. It supplies failure-mode-grounded questions and evidence boundaries only. A later audit must independently establish target identity, authority, configuration and current behaviour before answering them.
